iṅkilīṣ, pireṉc mutaliya pāṣikaḷip pōlat tamiḻil vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷ illāmaliruppatu peruṅkuṟi yeṉpati nām oppuk koḷkiṟōm. antak kuṟiyip parikarippataṟkākat tāṉ ellārum rāja pāṣikaḷum tamiḻum kalantu paṭikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpukiṟōm. rāja pāṣakaḷum cutēca pāṣikaḷum naṉṟāka uṇarntavarkaḷ maṭṭum uttamamāṉa vacaṉak kāviyaṅkaḷi eḻutak kūṭumēyallātu itararkaḷ eḻutak kūṭumā? vacaṉak kāviyaṅkaḷāl jaṉaṅkaḷ tiruntavēṇṭumē yallātu, ceyyuṭkaḷip paṭittut tiruntuvatu acāttiyamallavā? airōppiya pāṣikaḷil vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷ illāmalirukkumāṉāl antat tēcaṅkaḷ nākarikamum naṟpāṅkum aṭintirukkak kūṭumā? appaṭiyē nammuṭiya cuya pāṣikaḷil vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷ illāmal irukkiṟavariyil intat tēcam cariyāṉa cīrtiruttam aṭiyāteṉpatu niccayam. cutēca pāṣikaḷip paṭikkāmal rāja pāṣikaḷi maṭṭum paṭikkiṟavarkaḷ maṟṟa jaṉaṅkaḷik kalavāmal tāṅkaḷ oru anniya tēcattār pōl jīvikkiṟārkaḷ. rāja pāṣikaḷ teriyāta taṅkaḷuṭiya mātā pitākkaḷ maṉivi mintar mutaliyōrkaḷiṭattil pēcuvatu kūṭa avarkaḷukku aruvaṟuppā yirukkiṟatu. tāṅkaḷum cuya pāṣikaḷi naṉṟākap paṭikkāmalum maṟṟavarkaḷukkup pōtikkāmalum iruppatu avarkaḷuṭiya picakē yallāmal avarkaḷuṭiya pantu jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ picakallavē! rāja pāṣiyip paṭittuk kalviyiṉ arumi aṟintavarkaḷē cuya pāṣikaḷik kavaṉikkāmaliruppārkaḷāṉāl itara jaṉaṅkaḷ eppaṭi kavaṉikkak kūṭum? stirīkaḷum maṟṟa jaṉaṅkaḷum cuya pāṣikaḷip paṭittut tiruttavēṇṭumē yallātu avarkaḷ ellārum rāja pāṣikaḷik kaṟṟuṇarvatu cāttiyamāṉa kāriyamā? cuya pāṣiyik kallāmal rāja pāṣiyi maṭṭum paṭikkiṟavarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ maṭṭum piḻikka aṟivārkaḷēyaṉṟi maṟṟavarkaḷukku avarkaḷāl eṉṉa cātakam? cuya pāṣiyip paṭikkātavarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ keṭuvatu maṉṟi airōppiyarkaḷiyum keṭukkiṟārkaḷ. muṉ vanta airōpiyarkaḷ it tēca pāṣikaḷi evvaḷavō kavaṉamākap paṭittārkaḷ. ippotu cutēcikaḷē cuya pāṣikaḷik kiviṭṭapaṭiyāl airōpiyarkaḷum anta pāṣikaḷi apatārttamāka eṇṇukiṟārkaḷ. ivvāṟu nammuṭiya pāṣikaḷi anniyar avamatikkumpaṭi ceyvatu ayuktam allavā?

inta tēcattup periya pirapukkaḷ, taṉavāṉkaḷ, mirācutārarkaḷ, jamīṉtārkaḷ, pārivarttakarkaḷ mutalāṉavarkaḷuṭiya aṟiyāmiyi niṉikkumpōtu namakkup piralāpamum, perumūccum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷil anēkar cutta nirakṣarakukṣikaḷāyirukkiṟārkaḷ. cilar taṅkaḷuṭiya kiyeḻuttukkaḷi maṭṭum eḻutak kaṟṟukkoṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. “cuppiramaṇiyaṉ” eṉpataṟkuc “cukkiramaṇiyaṉ” eṉṟum “citamparam” eṉpataṟkuc “celamparam” eṉṟum, “turicāmi” eṉpataṟku “toricāmi” eṉṟum, “poṉṉampalam” eṉpataṟku “poṇṇampalam” eṉṟum, “vittiyaliṅkam” eṉpataṟku “vittiliṅkam” eṉṟum kiyeḻuttu vikkiṟārkaḷ. inta vitva cirōmaṇikaḷē, jūrikaḷākavum muṉicipal kamiṣaṉarkaḷākavum lōkal paṇṭu memparkaḷākavum, peṉcmējistirēṭṭukaḷākavum tēvālaya tarmālaya vicāraṇik karttākkaḷākavum (Trustees of temples and charitable institutions) niyamikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭiya atikāra stāṉaṅkaḷukkup pōkumpōtu, piratimikaḷip pōl nāṟkālikaḷil vīṟṟirukkiṟārkaḷē yallātu, avarkaḷuṭiya vēli iṉṉateṉpatip pariccētam aṟiyārkaḷ. piratimikaḷukkum ivarkaḷukkum pētam eṉṉaveṉṟāl, piratimikaḷ aciyāmalirukkiṉṟaṉa, ivarkaḷ nāṟkālikaḷil tūṅki viḻuntu acintukoṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. tēca kāviyaṅkaḷil takunta vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷ irukkumāṉāl ivvaḷavu nirppākkiyamāṉa stitiyiliruppārkaḷā? ātalāl iṅkilīṣ, pireṉc mutaliya rājapāṣikaḷip paṭikkiṟavarkaḷ, tēca pāṣikaḷiyun tīrkkamāka uṇarntu inta tēcattic cūḻntirukkiṟa aṟiyāmi yeṉṉum antakāram nīṅkumpaṭi vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷeṉṉum ñāṉa tīpaṅkaḷi ēṟṟuvārkaḷeṉṟu nampukiṟōm. tamiḻ paṭikkātavarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭil vacikka yōkkiyarkaḷ alla. avarkaḷ enta ūr pāṣiyip paṭikkiṟārkaḷō, anta ūrē avarkaḷukkut takunta iṭamākiyāl cuya pāṣiyip paṭikkāmal iṅkilīṣ maṭṭum paṭikkiṟavarkaḷi iṅkilīṣ tēcattukku aṉuppi viṭuvōm. pireṉc maṭṭum paṭikkiṟavarkaḷip pāris paṭṭaṇattukku aṉuppuvōm. lattīṉukkuñ camskirutattukkuñ conta ūr illātapaṭiyāl anta pāṣikaḷip paṭikkiṟavarkaḷi nām anāmakaraṇat tīvukku aṉuppuvōm” eṉṟāḷ.



* * *





43-ām atikāram

uttiyōka mamati—karvikaḷukku naṟputti



vikkiramapuriyil muntiṉa rājāvāl niyamikkappaṭṭa oru atikāri, karviṣṭaṉāyum parama tuṣṭaṉāyum iruntāṉ. “aṟpaṉukku aicuvariyam vantāl artta rāttiriyil kuṭi piṭippāṉ” eṉkiṟa paḻamoḻippaṭi avaṉukku atikāram kiṭittavuṭaṉē taṉṉuṭiya pūrva stitiyic cuttamāy maṟantu taṉṉi oru avatārap puruṣaṉ pōl eṇṇik koṇṭāṉ. vittiyilum, puttiyilum, taṉattilum, kulattilum taṉakkuc camāṉa māṉavarkaḷ oruvarum illi yeṉkiṟa akampāvamum, mamatiyum, uṭiyavaṉ āṉāṉ. avaṉ irukkiṟa iṭattil ī paṟakkak kūṭātu. eṟumpu ūrak kūṭātu, kuruvi kattak kūṭātu, oruvarum pēcak kūṭātu; eppōtum nicaptamāyirukka vēṇṭum. avaṉuṭiya vīṭṭukku etirē oruvarum jōṭu pōṭṭuk koṇṭu naṭakkak kūṭātu. aṅka vastiram pōṭak kūṭātu. kivīcikkoṇṭu naṭakkak kūṭātu. tāmpūlam tarikkak kūṭātu. ciṅkattiṉ kuki ōrattiṟ pōkiṟavarkaḷ payantu patuṅkik koṇṭu pōkiṟatu pōla, ivaṉ vīṭṭukku etirē pōkiṟavarkaḷum naṭuṅkik koṇṭu, nicaptamākap pōka vēṇṭum. avaṉ veḷiyē puṟappaṭṭāl, uṭkārntirukkiṟavarkaḷ ellārum eḻuntu viṭa vēṇṭum; naṭakkiṟavarkaḷ ellōrum niṉṟuviṭa vēṇṭum; cakalarum pūmiyilē viḻuntu cāṣṭāṅkamākat taṇṭam ceyyavēṇṭum. appaṭic ceyyātavarkaḷukku aparātaṅkaḷum, ākkiṉikaḷum kiṭippatu cittamē. avaṉik kaṇṭa vuṭaṉē, jōṭu pōṭṭuk koṇṭiruppavarkaḷ ellārum, avikaḷik kaḻaṟṟukiṟa vēkattip pārttāl avaṉi aṭippataṟkākavē kaḻaṟṟukiṟatu pōlat tōṉṟum. āṉāl vāstavattil mariyātikkāka jōṭukaḷik kaḻaṟṟukiṟārkaḷē yaṉṟi, avaṉi aṭippataṟkāka alla. avaṉ teruvil naṭakkumpōtu teru muḻutum taṉakkē contam pōla aṭittuk koṇṭu kāloru pakkam, kiyoru pakkam, vēṣṭiyoru pakkam, tāṉ oru pakkamāka viṟittuk koṇṭu naṭappāṉ. naṭakkum pōtu avaṉuṭiya jōṭu, avaṉip paṭīr paṭīreṉṟu aṭittuk koṇṭē pōkum. avaṉi maṭṭum ellōrum vaṇaṅka vēṇṭumē tavira, avaṉ ākācattip pārttuk koṇṭu naṭakkiṟatē yallātu evariyum vaṇaṅkukiṟa tilli. avaṉ iṟumāppuṭaṉ pūmiyip pārttu naṭakkiṟa tilli. avaṉ kālilē viṣam tīṇṭiṉālum kuṉintu pārkkiṟatilli. taliyilē vācaṟpaṭi iṭittālum kuṉikiṟatilli.



avaṉ atikāri stāṉattil irukkumpōtu, jaṉaṅkaḷ ellōruṅ ki kaṭṭik koṇṭum, vēṣṭikaḷit tūkkik kaṭṭikkoṇṭum, tūrattil niṟka vēṇṭumēyallātu, avaṉ camīpattil oruvarum neruṅkak kūṭātu. avaṉ kaṇṇālē oruvariyum ēṟeṭuttup pārkkiṟa tilli. avaṉuṭiya vāyilē, tiṭṭukkaḷum utācīṉaṅkaḷum puṟappaṭumē yallātu, nalla vārttikaḷ puṟappaṭukiṟa tilli. pēykkuk kaḷ vārttatu pōla avaṉuṭiya mukattil eppōtum kōpam kūttāṭik koṇṭirukkiṟatē yallātu, poṟumiyāka oru kāriyattiyum vicārikkiṟa tilli. avaṉi yār atikamāka vaṇaṅkit tappu stōttiram ceykiṟārkaḷō, avarkaḷ pakṣam tīrmāṉikkiṟatē yaṉṟi uṇmiyik kaṇṭupiṭittut tīrmāṉikkiṟa tilli. nāṉ oru nāḷ māṟu vēṣam pōṭṭukkoṇṭu avaṉuṭiya atikāra stāṉattup pōy, maṟṟa jaṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tūrattil niṉṟu koṇṭiruntēṉ. avaṉi ēmāṟṟit tīrppup peṟṟuk koḷvataṟkāka, avaṉi oru vaḻakkāḷi stōttirañ ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. eppaṭiyeṉṟāl “mahāp pirapuvē! maṇṭalātipatiyē! inta pañca cāṣṭak kōṭi pūmaṇṭalattil, uṅkaḷukkuc camamāka yārirukkiṟārkaḷ? taṉattilē nīṅkaḷ kupēraṉ, vittiyilē nīṅkaḷ āticēṣaṉ, puttiyilē pirahaspati, aḻakilē maṉmataṉ; cākṣāt kaṭavuḷ nīṅkaḷē. atu pēcāt teyvam; nīṅkaḷ pēcum teyvam. atu appirattiyakṣam; nīṅkaḷ pirattiyakṣamāṉa teyvam. ippōtu, nīṅkaḷ niṉittāl anēkaṅ kuṭikaḷi vāḻvikkalām; anēkak kuṭikaḷik keṭuttu viṭalām. antat teyvattiṉālē appaṭic ceyyamuṭiyumā?” eṉṟu palavārāka mukastuti pēcikkoṇṭiruntāṉ. anta atikāriyum taṉṉit teyvameṉṟē maṉaskarittuk koṇṭu, puṉṉakiyuṭaṉ ācaṉattil uṭkārntiruntāṉ. appōtu, oru koḷḷit tēḷ eppaṭiyō vantu atikāriyi naṟukkeṉṟu kaṭittu viṭṭatu. uṭaṉē anta pirattiyakṣamāṉa teyvam kīḻē viḻuntu, kaṭa kaṭaveṉṟu uruḷa ārampittu. vittiyarkaḷum māntirīkarkaḷum vantu kūṭiviṭṭārkaḷ. nāṉ teyvattit tēḷ koṭṭumā? eṉṟu collik koṇṭu araṇmaṉikkup pōy naṭanta caṅkatikaḷi yellām ñāṉāmpāḷukku viḷampiṉatumaṉṟi, anta atikāriyi oru nāḷ aḻippittut taṉimiyāka vittuk koṇṭu piṉ varumāṟu avaṉukkup putti coṉṉēṉ.

“maṉuṣaṉ taṉṉit tāṉē aṟivāṉāṉāl, avaṉ oru nāḷuṅ karvappaṭa māṭṭāṉ. nammuṭiya tēkattiṉ acuttaṅkaḷiyum nammuṭiya ācāpācaṅkaḷiyum turkkuṇaṅkaḷiyum tuṣkiruttiyaṅkaḷiyum citta vikāraṅkaḷiyum namakku uṇṭākiṟa viyātikaḷiyum turppalaṅkaḷiyum tēka anittiyattiyum maraṇattiyum nām yōcippōmāṉāl, nām veṭkappaṭṭut talikuṉiya vēṇṭiyatēyallāmal, karvappaṭukiṟataṟku eṉṉa iṭamirukkiṟatu? nām acuttamāṉa karppattil uṟpattiyāki acuttattilē piṟantu acuttattilē vaḷarntu akaciyamāṉa patārttaṅkaḷiyē pucittu acuciyākavē jīvittu acuciyākavē iṟantu pōkiṟōm. irattamum māmicamum elumpum narampum malajalātiyaṅkaḷuṅkūṭiya nammuṭiya tēkam acuttamēyaṉṟi vēṟallavē. kaṇṇilē pīḷi kātilē kuṟumpi mūkkilē caḷi vāyilē eccil taliyilē pēṉ tēkamuḻutun turnāṟṟam ippaṭiyāka muḻutum acuttamāka irukkiṟatu. nām aṭikkaṭi tēkattik kaḻuvāviṭṭāl nammuṭiya turkkantam namakkē cakikkumā?

nammuṭiya vāḻvun tāḻvum cuvāmiyiṉuṭiya kiyil irukkiṉṟaṉavē yallātu, nammuṭiya svātīṉattil eṉṉa irukkiṟatu? aṭutta nimiṣattil iṉṉatu varumeṉpatu namakkut teriyumā? nammuṭiya āstikaḷi niṉittuk karvappaṭuvōmāṉāl, anta āstikaḷum anittiyam; avikaḷi anupavikkiṟa nāmum anittiyarkaḷā yirukkumpōtu nām eppaṭi karvappaṭak kūṭum? nām piṟakkumpōtu oru kōvaṇattukkuk kūṭa vaḻiyillāmal cutta nirvāṇikaḷāyp piṟantu nirvāṇikaḷāyiṟantu pōkiṟōm. nām piṟakkumpōtu oru āstiyiyum nām kūṭak koṇṭu vantatumilli; kūṭak koṇṭupōvatumilli. nām anupavikkiṟa poruḷkaḷ ettaṉi nāḷ nammōṭu kūṭiyirukku meṉpatum niccayamilliyē! nammuṭiya kalviyip paṟṟi nām iṟumāppu aṭivōmāṉāl, atu namakkup piṟar pōtittatēyaṉṟi nām piṟakkumpōtu nammōṭukūṭap piṟantatallavē! allāmalum nammuṭiya taliyi mitikkumpaṭiyāṉa kalvimāṉkaḷ ulakattil anēkar irukkavilliyā? evaṉ taṉṉit tāṉē uyarttikkoḷkiṟāṉō avaṉ tāḻttap paṭuvāṉ. evaṉ taṉṉittāṉē tāḻttukiṟāṉō avaṉ uyarttap paṭuvāṉ” eṉpatu vēta vākkiyamallavā?

jaṉaṅkaḷuṭiya naṉmikkāka atikārikaḷ niyamikkappaṭṭu, jaṉaṅkaḷuṭiya campaḷaṅkaḷiyē atikārikaḷ vāṅkic cāppiṭukiṟapaṭiyāl atikārikaḷ jaṉaṅkaḷukku ūḻiyakkārarkaḷē yallātu ejamāṉkaḷ allaveṉpati avarkaḷ eppōtum ñāpakattil vikkavēṇṭum. appaṭi eṇṇāmal jaṉaṅkaḷit taṅkaḷukku aṭimikaḷip pōla eṇṇukiṟa atikārikaḷ akkiramikaḷ allavā? nīr mēṟkulameṉṟu perumi pārāṭṭikkoṇṭu maṟṟavarkaḷi yellām īṉa jātikaḷeṉṟum ēḻikaḷeṉṟum aṭikkaṭi tūṣippatākak kēḷvippaṭukiṟōm. kuṇamum puttiyum, cirēṣṭamē tavira, jātipētaṅkaḷum antastukkaḷum maṉuṣarkaḷuṭiya kaṭṭuppāṭu eṉpati nīr aṟiyāmaṟpōṉatu periya āccariyam allavā?

nalla marattilē pulluruvi pāyntatu pōl, oru naṟkuṇamuṭiya aracaṉukkut turkkuṇamuḷḷa puttiraṉ oruvaṉ iruntāṉ. avaṉ taṉṉi mikavum uyarvāka eṇṇi maṟṟavarkaḷit tāḻvāka naṭatti vantāṉ. avaṉit tiruttuvataṟkāka rājā evvaḷavu pirayācip paṭṭum avaṉ tiruntavilli. anta rājakumāraṉukku vivākamāki oru kuḻanti piṟantatu. antap piḷḷikkum appōtu piṟanta vēṟoru ēḻip piḷḷikkum orē mātiriyāka uṭi uṭutti iraṇṭu piḷḷikaḷiyum orē iṭattil vittirukkumpaṭi, rājā tiṭṭañ ceytiruntār. anta rājakumāraṉ taṉ piḷḷiyip pārkkiṟataṟkāka ōṭiṉa pōtu iraṇṭu piḷḷikaḷik kaṇṭu taṉ piḷḷi iṉṉateṉṟu teriyāmal vēlikkārarkaḷik kōpittuk koṇṭāṉ. anta camayattil rājā vantu taṉ puttiraṉip pārttu makaṉē! mēṉmiyāṉa antastum uyarnta rattamum uḷḷa uṉ piḷḷiyi nī kaṇṭu koḷḷak kūṭātā? ēṉ mayaṅkukiṟāy? eṉṟār. rājakumāraṉukku nāṇamuṇṭāki tali kaviḻntu koṇṭu atō mukamāka niṉṟāṉ. uṭaṉē antap piḷḷikaḷil oru piḷḷiyit toṭṭukkāṭṭi “itu tāṉ uṉ piḷḷi. intap piḷḷiyiṉ kālil oru nāṭāvik kaṭṭi vittiruntēṉ. appaṭic ceyyāviṭṭāl uṉ piḷḷi iṉṉateṉṟu teriyātu. piṟakkumpōtu ellōrum camāṉameṉṟum uyarkulamum mēmpāṭum cuttak kaṟpitameṉṟum aṟintukoḷ” eṉṟār.

anta rājakumāraṉum oru vēlikkāraṉum viyātiyā yirunta pōtu, vittiyar vantu pārviyiṭṭu iruvarukkum rattaṅ kutti vāṅkumpaṭi coṉṉār. antappaṭi iruvaruṭiya rattamum eṭuttu vevvēṟu pāttiraṅkaḷil vikkappaṭṭiruntatu. anta rājā taṉṉuṭiya kumāraṉ kūṭa irukkumpōtu vittiyari aḻittu anta iraṇṭu pāttiraṅkaḷum irukkiṟa rattaṅkaḷil etu nalla rattameṉṟu paricōtittut terivikkumpaṭi coṉṉār. uṭaṉē rājā taṉ kumāraṉip pārttu nammuṭiya vamcamum rattamum mēlāṉatu eṉṟu eṇṇikkoṇṭiruntōm. uṉṉuṭiya rattattip pārkkilum vēlikkāraṉuṭiya rattam mēlāyiruppatāka vittiyar collukiṟār. avaṉ nammip pōl kaṇṭa patārttaṅkaḷiyellām pucikkāmal mita pōjaṉam ceykiṟapaṭiyālum, avaṉ tēkappirayācip paṭṭu jīvikkiṟa paṭiyālum, avaṉuṭiya rattam atika cuttamāy irukkiṟatu. appaṭiyirukka nammuṭiya rattam mēlāṉateṉṟu nām akampāvam aṭivatu takumā?” eṉṟār.

oru ūril oru periya pirapuviṉuṭiya cikarattukkuc camīpattil oru nāṇaṟkāṭu iruntatu. atu kūṭi piṉṉiviṟkiṟa oru ēḻikkuc contamāka iruntatu. antak kāṭṭi vilikku vāṅkavēṇṭumeṉṟu antap pirapu muyaṟci ceytāṉ. anta ēḻikku antak kāṭṭit tavira vēṟu jīvaṉattukku mārkkamillātapaṭiyāl kāṭṭi vilikkuk koṭukka nirākarittāṉ. antap pirapuvukkuk kōpamuṇṭāki, anta nāṇalkaḷil neruppu vittu nirmūlamākki anta ēḻiyiyum aṭittu upattiravañ ceytāṉ. eḷiyavaṉ rājāviṉiṭattil muṟiyiṭṭuk koṇṭataṉāl rājā pirapuvi varavaḻittu vicāraṇi ceytār. pirapu aracaṉip pārttu, anta aṟpap payal eṉṉuṭiya kuravatti evvaḷavum matikkāmaṟ pōyviṭṭatāl avaṉi nāṉ aṭittatu kiramantāṉ eṉṟāṉ. aracaṉ pirapuvip pārttu uṉṉuṭiya muppāṭṭaṉ viṟaku veṭṭik kālakṣēpañ ceytuvantāṉ. piṉpu avaṉ paṭivīraṉāki curiya parākkiramaṅ kāṭṭiyapaṭiyāl eṉṉuṭiya pāṭṭaṉārukkuc cantōṣamuṇṭāki, avaṉi mēmpaṭuttit tiraviyavantaṉ ākkiṉāṉ. uṉṉuṭiya muppāṭṭaṉ ātiyil viṟakut taliyaṉāyirunta pōtilum, piṟaku taṉṉuṭiya cuya cāmarttiyattiṉāl mēṉmiyaṭintāṉ. nī avaṉ tēṭiṉa āstiyi vittuk koṇṭu cuya yōkkiyatiyillāmal kālaṅ kaḻikkiṟāy eṉṟār. piṟaku aracaṉ oru mālumiyip pārttu ivarkaḷ iruvariyum oru kappalil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu pōy oru tīvil nirvāṇamāy viṭṭuviṭu. ivarkaḷil evaṉ camarttaṉ eṉpati aṟivōm eṉṟār. uṭaṉē antap paṭi niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭatu. antat tīvil antap pirapu vastiramillāmal paṉiyiluṅ kuḷirilum paṭṭa avasti maraṇāvastikkuc camāṉamāyiruntatu. anta ēḻikku oru kaṣṭamum tōṉṟavilli. avaṉ cila ceṭikaḷip piṭuṅki nār urittut taṉakkum antap pirapuvukkum vastirañ ceytukoṇṭāṉ. antat tīvil vacikkiṟa anākarikamāṉa kāṭṭu jaṉaṅkaḷukku, anta eḷiyavaṉ kūṭi mutalāṉatu piṉṉik koṭuttapaṭiyāl avarkaḷukkuc cantōṣamuṇṭāki avaṉukku uṇavu mutaliya patārttaṅkaḷ koṭuttārkaḷ. avaṉ tāṉum pucittu anta veṟum pirapuvukkuñ cāppāṭuṅ koṭuttu rakṣittāṉ. antap pirapu oru vēliyuñ ceyyāmal cummāyiruppati anta milēccarkaḷ aṟintu avaṉik kolla ārampittārkaḷ. appōtu anta eḷiyavaṉ cipāricu ceytu pirapuvi viṭuvittup pirāṇap piratiṣṭi ceyvittāṉ. uṭaṉē pirapuvukku ñāṉōtayam uṇṭāki, anta eḷiyavaṉ illāviṭṭāl antat, tīvē taṉakku mayāṉap pūmiyāka irukkumeṉṟu terintu koṇṭāṉ. cila nāḷikkup piṟaku rājā anta iruvariyuṅ koṇṭuvarumpaṭi uttaravu ceytār. rājā muṉpāka antap pirapu taṉṉuṭiya aṟiyāmiyiyum eḷiyavaṉ taṉakkuc ceyta upakāraṅkaḷiyum oppukkoṇṭu taṉṉuṭiya āstikaḷiṟ pātiyi anta ēḻikkup paṅkiṭṭuk koṭuttāṉ. rājā antap pirapuvip pārtṭuc collukiṟār:- anta ēḻi illāviṭṭaṭāl antak kāṉakatti viṭṭu nī vāṉakattukkup pōyiruppāyeṉpatu niccayam. appaṭiyē cakala tēcaṅkaḷil irukkiṟa pirapukkaḷum ēḻikaḷāl jīvikkiṟārkaḷē yallāmal maṟṟappaṭiyalla. eṇṇikkiyillāta ēḻikaḷuṭiya tēkap pirayācattiṉāl namakkuc cakala pākkiyaṅkaḷuṅ kiṭikkiṟa paṭiyāl avarkaḷi nām periya upakārikaḷāka matikka vēṇṭum eṉṟār.

oru pirapu taṉakkup piḷḷi piṟanta uṭaṉē pāl koṭuppataṟkāka antap piḷḷiyip pāṟkāri kiyil oppuvittār. antappiḷḷiyum pāṟkāri piḷḷiyum camāṉa vayatākavum apētamākavum iruntapaṭiyāl antap pāṟkāri taṉ piḷḷiyip pirapu piḷḷiyākavum pirapuviṉ piḷḷiyit taṉ piḷḷiyākavum māṟṟi viṭṭāḷ. intap pirakāram pāṟkārip piḷḷi pirapuvākavum, pirapu vīṭṭup piḷḷi ēḻiyākavum māṟippōy viṭṭārkaḷ. cila pirapukkaḷ akampāvattiṉāl anniyariṭattil vāyik koṭuttu avamāṉappaṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. appaṭippaṭṭa cila tiruṣṭāntaṅkaḷit terivikkiṟēṉ.

oru aracaṉ cila anniya tēcattu varttakarkaḷiṭattil cila kutirikaḷi vilikku vāṅkikkoṇṭu, piṉṉum iraṇṭu lakṣa rūpāy avarkaḷ kiyil atikamākak koṭuttu antap paṇattukkuḷḷa kutirikaḷik koṇṭu varumpaṭi coṉṉāṉ. anta viyapārikaḷ paṇatti vāṅkik koṇṭupōyviṭṭārkaḷ. piṟaku oru nāḷ anta rājā taṉ mantiriyi aḻittu taṉ tēcattil uḷḷa mūṭarkaḷuṭiya peyurkaḷiyellām eḻutik koṇṭu varumpaṭi ākñāpittāṉ. mantiri rājāvip pārttu nāṉ muṉṉamē antappaṭi oru aṭṭavaṇi eḻuti vittirukkiṟēṉ. atil ellārukkum munti uṅkaḷuṭiya peyari eḻutiyirukkiṟēṉ. ēṉeṉṟāl anta varttakarkaḷ iṉṉa ūreṉṟu terintukoḷḷāmalum avarkaḷiṭattil jāmīṉ vāṅkāmalum iraṇṭu lakṣa rūpāy avarkaḷ kiyil nīṅkaḷ koṭuttuviṭṭatāl mūṭaruṭiya jāptāvil mutaṉmiyāka uṅkaḷuṭiya peyari eḻutiyirukkiṟēṉ eṉṟāṉ. aracaṉ mantiriyip pārttu  avarkaḷ kutirikaḷik koṇṭu vantāl appōtu eṉṉa ceyvāy? eṉṟāṉ. avarkaḷ kutirikaḷik koṇṭu vantāl uṅkaḷ peyarik kiṟukkiviṭṭu avarkaḷ peyarip patittukkoḷḷuvēṉ eṉṟu mantiri pirati uttirañ coṉṉāṉ.

oruvaṉukkup periya uttiyōkaṅ kiṭittapaṭiyāl avaṉuṭiya cinēkitaṉ avaṉukku maṅkaḷa vārtti colvataṟkāka vantāṉ. avaṉ taṉṉuṭiya uttiyōka mamatiyāl ciṉēkitaṉip pārttu nī yār? eṉṟu viṉāviṉāṉ. ciṉēkitaṉukku kōpam jaṉittu nāṉ uṉṉuṭiya paḻiya nēcaṉ. uṉakku iraṇṭu kaṇṇum avintu pōṉatāka kēḷvippaṭṭu tukkaṅ koṇṭāṭa uṉṉiṭattukku vantēṉ eṉṟāṉ.

oru niyāyātipati oru cākṣikkāraṉip pārttu  “nī tiruṭaṉeṉṟu kaṇṇāṭipōla uṉ mukaṅ kāṭṭukiṟatu” eṉṟāṉ. uṭaṉē cākṣikkāraṉ antak keṭṭa niyātipatiyip pārttu  “eṉṉuṭiya, mukam kaṇṇāṭi yāṉatāl, itil uṅkaḷ mukattik kāṇukiṟīrkaḷ” eṉṟāṉ. ākiyāl niyāyātipati tiruṭaṉeṉpatāyiṟṟu.

oru vaḻakkāḷi niyāyacapiyiṟ pēcikoṇṭirukkum pōtu avaṉi, avaṉuṭiya etiriyiṉ vakkīl pārttu ”nī ēṉ nāy pōṟ kurikkiṉṟāy?” eṉṟāṉ. ”tiruṭṭup payalip pārttāl, nāy kurikkātā?” eṉṟu anta vaḻakkāḷi maṟumoḻi coṉṉāṉ. itaṉāl vakkīlit tiruṭaṉ ākkiviṭṭāṉ.

oru aracaṉum avaṉuṭiya makaṉum oru vikaṭakaviyi aḻittukkoṇṭu vēṭṭikkup pōṉārkaḷ. vēṭṭi āṭik koṇṭirukkumpōtu maḻi piṭittukkoṇṭu, rājāvum avar makaṉum naṉintu pōyviṭṭārkaḷ; naṉintu pōṉa avarkaḷuṭiya uṭuppukkaḷik kaḻaṟṟi, oru mūṭṭiyākak kaṭṭi vikaṭakavi talimēlē vittārkaḷ. avaṉ tūkkikkoṇṭu pōkum pōtu, ”irājāvum avar makaṉum, ”vikaṭakavi oru kaḻutip pāram cumantu koṇṭu pōkiṟāṉ” eṉṟu parikācamākap pēcikkoṇṭu pōṉārkaḷ. vikaṭakavi avarkaḷit tirumpi pārttu ”oru kaḻuti pārantāṉā? iraṇṭu kaḻutip pārañ cumakkiṟēṉ”, eṉṟu colli irājāviyum avar makaṉiyum kaḻutikaḷ ākkiviṭṭāṉ.

tarittira ṉāṉa oru vittuvāṉ, oru taṉavāṉ vīṭṭukkup pōy, avaṉukkum taṉakkum oru cāṇ tūram irukkum paṭiyāṉa camīpattil uṭkārntāṉ. antat taṉavāṉ kōpaṅkoṇṭu vittuvāṉip pārttu, “kaḻutikkum uṉakkum eṉṉa vittiyācam?” eṉṟāṉ. vittuvāṉ ”oru cāṇtāṉ vittiyācam” eṉṟu aḷantu kāṭṭiṉāṉ.

oru aracaṉ poḻutu viṭiyumuṉ eḻuntu vēṭṭiyāṭuvataṟkāka kāṭṭukkup pōṉāṉ. kāṭṭil mattiyāṉam varikkuñ cuṟṟittirintum vēṭṭi akappaṭavilli. aṉṟiyatiṉam viṭiyaṟkālattil aracaṉ oru kuṭiyāṉavaṉ mukattil viḻittapaṭiyāl atu nimittan taṉakku vēṭṭi akappaṭavilliyeṉṟu niṉittu, antak kuṭiyāṉavaṉik koṉṟuviṭumpaṭi uttiravu ceytāṉ. antak kuṭiyāṉavaṉ aracaṉip pārttu, ”makārājāvē! pirātakkālattil nīṅkaḷ eṉ mukattil viḻittēṉ. eṉ mukattil nīṅkaḷ viḻittataṟku uṅkaḷukku vēṭṭi akappaṭavilli. uṅkaḷ mukattil nāṉ viḻittataṟku eṉṉuṭiya pirāṇaṉi iḻantu pōkum paṭi campavit tirukkiṟatu” eṉṟāṉ. itik kēṭṭavuṭaṉē aracaṉukku vivēkam uṇṭāki ”kuṭiyāṉavaṉik kollavēṇṭam” eṉṟu uttiravu ceytāṉ.

oru vakkīl oru niyāya capiyil, oru niyāya vātam ceytu koṇṭirukkum pōtu, niyāyātipati, “kaḻuti kattukiṟatu kaḻuti kattukiṟatu” eṉṟu vāykkuḷḷē muṇumuṇuttuk koṇṭiruntār. ati vakkīl kēṭṭuṅ kēḷātavar pōlat taṉ kakṣiyip pēciṉār. piṟaku niyāyātipāti tīrmāṉam collik koṇṭirukkumpōtu oru yatārttamāṉa kaḻuti teruvilē kattat tuvaṅkiṟṟu. uṭaṉē niyātipati tīrmāṉam colvati niṟutti, ”atu eṉṉa captam?” eṉṟār. anta vakkīl eḻuntu, ”atu kōrṭṭā ruṭiya etiroli tāṉ, vēṟoṉṟum alla” eṉṟār. niyātipati atōmukam āṉār.

oruvaṉi oruvaṉ aṭitta caṅkatiyip paṟṟi niyāya capiyil vicāraṇi naṭantapōtu piriyātukkāraṉik kuṟṟavāḷi aṭittatit tāṉ pārttatāka oru cākk kāraṉ coṉṉāṉ. kuṟṟavāḷiyiṉ vakkīl antac cākṣikkāraṉip pārttu “anta aṭi eppaṭippaṭṭa aṭi?” eṉṟu kēṭṭār. kiyi ōṅkip palamāka aṭittākac cākṣikkāraṉ coṉṉāṉ. vakkīl avaṉip pārttu ”atu ippaṭippaṭṭa aṭiyeṉṟu eṉakku nī meyppikka vēṇṭum” eṉṟāṉ. cākṣikkāraṉ kōrṭṭārip pārttu ”atu ippaṭippaṭṭa aṭiyeṉṟu nāṉ eppaṭi meyppippēṉ? atu palamāṉa aṭitāṉ” eṉṟāṉ. niyāyātipati cākṣikkāraṉip pārttu ”vakkīl collukiṟapaṭi avarukku nī meyppikkattāṉ vēṇṭum” eṉṟāṉ. uṭaṉē cākṣikkāraṉ iraṇṭu kikiḷiyum ōṅki taṉ palamellām kūṭṭi vakkīli aṭittu ”iv vakiyākattāṉ kuṟṟavāḷi aṭittāṉ,” eṉṟāṉ. vakkīl anta aṭi poṟukkamāṭṭāmal kīḻē viḻuntuviṭṭār. itu vakkīliṉuṭiya svayaṅkiruta aparātamāṉatāl cākṣikkāraṉi oṉṟuñ ceyyakkūṭāmaṟ pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟēṉ.



* * *





44-ām atikāram

āṇpāl peṇpāl mayakkam—

peṇṇip peṇ virumpal—

irājāṅka pāramparip pāttiram



vikkiramapuri kuṭiyaracāvataṟku munti, ati āṇṭuvanta aracaṉukkup purukṣappiraji yilli yeṉpati muṉṉamē terivittirukkiṟēṉ. avarukku atirūpa ceḷantariyamāṉa oru peṇ kuḻanti maṭṭum iruntatu. antap peṇ peyar ānanta valli antak kuḻanti atipālliyamāyirukkum pōtu, tāyum takappaṉumiṟantu pōy viṭṭatāl, pāṭṭiyāruṭiya kiyilē vaḷarntatu. ñāṉāmpāḷukkup paṭṭāpiṣēkamāṉa piṟaku, antak kuḻantiyit taṉ kuḻantipōṟ pāvittu, mikunta aṉpōṭuṅ karicaṉattōṭum ātarittuvantāḷ. takunta upāttiyāyarkaḷik koṇṭu vittiyāppiyācañ ceyvittatu miṉṟi, taṉṉāṟ kūṭiya pōtum, antap peṇṇukkuc caṉmārkkaṅkaḷiyum, rāja nītikiḷiyum ñāṉāmpāḷ pōtittu vantāḷ. antap peṇṇukkup pakkuvakālañ camīpitta uṭaṉē, avaḷukkum eṅkaḷukkum muka taricaṉa millāmal antappura vācamā yiruntāḷ. avaḷuṭiya antastuk kuriya kāriyaṅkaḷil oru kuṟivumillāmal, cakala mēmpāṭukaḷum upacāra mariyātikaḷum naṭantu vantaṉa.

ñāṉāmpāḷ āṇvēṭam pūṇṭukoṇṭu aracu ceyvatu taṉakku arikaṇṭamā yiruppatāl, tāṉ peṇpāl eṉpati jaṉaṅkaḷukkut terivikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu aṉēka āvartti apēkṣittāḷ. nāṉ kūṭāteṉṟu taṭuttapaṭiyāl eṉ piriyattukkāka avaṉ āṇ vēṣattuṭaṉ aracu ceytu vantāḷ. āṉāl rājjiyapārattic cērntu pala kavalikaḷiṉālum taṉṉuṭiya uṟṟār peṟṟārip pirintirukkiṟa ēkkattiṉālum ñāṉāmpāḷ cilanāḷāyc cantōṣamāyirāmal tēkam melintu pōṉāḷ. avaḷ orunāḷ eṉṉi nōkki neṭṭuyirppuṭaṉ collukiṟāḷ:- eppaṭippaṭṭa muḻumūṭcukkaḷāyiruntālum pirapañca māyāvikārattil matimayaṅkātiruppatu atiturlapameṉṟu periyōrkaḷ collukiṟārkaḷ. namakkup pirapañca māyiyuṭaṉē kūṭa rājayōkam vantuviṭṭatāl nām cittam pētittu nammuṭiya uṟṟār peṟṟārkaḷiyellām maṟantuviṭṭōm. nām avarkaḷi maṟantu viṭṭatu pōl avarkaḷ nammi oru nimiṣamāvatu maṟantiruppārkaḷā? nām ātiyūri viṭṭut tappip pōṉa camācāram kēḷvippaṭṭa uṭaṉē, avarkaḷ pirāṇaṉiyum vittiruppārkaḷā? avarkaḷi iṉṉoru taraṅ kāṇpōmā? avarkaḷuṭiya amiruta vācakattik kēṭpōmā? eṉṉuṭiya attiyārip pōlap puṇṇiyavatikaḷi nāṉ enta ulakattilē kāṇappōkiṟēṉ? eṉṟu colli muttumālipōṟ kaṇṇīr viṭṭut tēmpiṉāḷ. atik kēṭṭavuṭaṉē eṉakkuñ cakikkak kūṭāta cañcalam uṇṭākic ciṟitu nēram porumiṉēṉ. maṟupaṭiyum ñāṉāmpāḷ eṉṉip pārttu uṅkaḷuṭiya piriyattukkāka ituvariyum apāramāṉa inta rājāṅka pārattic cumantēṉ. iṉi mēṟ cumakka eṉṉāl muṭiyātu. irukkiṟa iṭan teriyāmal evvaḷavō aṭakka oṭukkamāyirukka vēṇṭiya stirī jātiyākiya nāṉ puruṣavēṣam pūṇṭukoṇṭu ettaṉi nāḷikkuk kaṣṭappaṭuvēṉ? iṉi eṉṉāl nirvakikkac cāttiyamillātapaṭiyāl nīṅkaḷ eṉ talimēlē tūkkivitta pāratti iṟakkiviṭumpaṭi kirupi ceyyap pirārttikkiṟēṉ eṉṟāḷ nāṉ avaḷip pārttu “nāṉ ceyya vēṇṭiya kāriyam iṉṉateṉṟu coṉṉāl uṭaṉē antappaṭi ceykiṟēṉ” eṉṟēṉ.

ñāṉāmpāḷ eṉṉip pārttu “nammuṭiya ūril namakku eṉṉa pākkiyaṅ kuṟivāyirukkiṟatu? inta ūrk kuṭikaḷuṭiya cukkiyattukkāka nām inta aracākṣiyatti ēṟṟuk koṇṭatē yallātu, namakku ētēṉum lāpam uṇṭā? kuṭikaḷukku vēṇṭiya cukkiyaṅkaḷiyum caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷiyum nām ēṟpaṭuttiviṭṭa paṭiyāl, iṉi mēl inta ūri āḷukiṟavarkaḷukku atikap pirayācam irātu. inta ūri muṉṉē āṇṭa rājāviṉ puttiriyākiya ānantavalli kūṭiya variyiṟ kalvi kaṟṟu kuṇacāliyākavum paṭṭattukku yōkkiyamāyum iruppatāl, avaḷukkup paṭṭāpiṣēkañ ceyvittu nām nammuṭiya ūrukkup pōvatu naṉmiyeṉṟu niṉikkiṟēṉ” eṉṟāḷ. nāṉ ñāṉāmpāḷip pārttu 'uṉṉuṭiya iṣṭappaṭi naṭakka eṉṉāl āṭaṅkamilli. āṉāl, anta jaṉaṅkaḷ anta rājaputtirikku makuṭañ cūṭṭac cammatippārkaḷō, cammatiyārkaḷō teriyavilli. aṉṟiyum inta nāṭu malikaḷālum camuttiraṅkaḷālum cūḻappaṭṭiruppatāl nammuṭiya ūrukku enta mārkkamāyp pōkiṟateṉṟun teriyavilli. anta vivaraṅkaḷellān terintukoṇṭu piṟaku anta rāja kaṉṉikikku makuṭāpiṣēkañ ceyvippatip paṟṟi yōcikkalām. atu variyil nammuṭiya eṇṇam piṟar aṟiyātapaṭi rakaciyamāyirukka vēṇṭum. rāyarum appājiyum aracāṇṭa kālattil ṭillip pāccā orē mātiriyāṉa mūṉṟu vikkirakaṅkaḷi aṉuppi avikaḷiṉ tāratammiyaṅkaḷit terivikkumpaṭi nirupam aṉuppiṉāṉ. mūṉṟum orē taṉmiyāyiruntapaṭiyāl avikaḷiṉ uyarvu tāḻvu teriyāmal ellōrum mayaṅkiṉārkaḷ. appāji anta vikkirakaṅkaḷiṉ kātuttoḷi vaḻiyāka īrkkukaḷi viṭṭup pārttāṉ. oru vikkirakattiṉ kātilē viṭṭa īrkku, maṟṟoru kātu vaḻiyākap puṟappaṭṭatu. iṉṉoru vikkirakattukku vāy vaḻiyākap puṟappaṭṭatu. maṟṟoru vikkirakattiṉ kātu vaḻiyāy viṭṭa īrkku, veḷiyē varāmal uḷḷē taṅkiviṭṭatu. anta mūṉṟāvatu vikkirakam pōla evaṉ rakaciyaṅkaḷi veḷiyē viṭāmal uḷḷē aṭakkukiṟāṉō, avaṉ uttamaṉeṉṟum, evarkaḷ kātiṉāl kēṭṭati veḷiyē viṭṭu viṭukiṟārkaḷō, avarkaḷ mattimarum atamareṉṟum appāji poruḷ viṭuvittāṉ. appaṭip pōl nāmum rakaciyaṅ kāppāṟṟavēṇṭum eṉṟēṉ. nāṉ coṉṉatu cariyeṉṟu ñāṉāmpāḷum aṅkīkarittuk koṇṭāḷ.

ataṟkuc cilanāḷikkup piṉpu oru nāṭkāliyil, mantiri piratāṉikaḷ mutaliya periya uttiyōkastarkaḷum, periya pirapukkaḷum, iṉṉum anēka jaṉaṅkaḷum, araṇmaṉiyil vantu rājacēvikkuk kāttiruppatākak kēḷvippaṭṭu, nāṉum ñāṉāmpāḷum eḻuntupōy vantavarkaḷukkup pēṭṭi koṭuttōm. avarkaḷ peruṅ kūṭṭamāy vantiruntapaṭiyāl, nāṅkaḷ āccariyam aṭintu eṉṉa vicēṣam? eṉṟu viṉaviṉōm. vayōtikarkaḷāṉa cila pirapukkaḷ eḻuntu ñāṉāmpāḷip pārttu maṇṭalēcvarā! makiparipālā!! nāṅkaḷ oru periya kāriyatti uttēcittu vantirukkiṟōm. tāṅkaḷ oru ākṣēpamuñ collāmal eṅkaḷuṭiya maṉōratatti niṟivēṟṟavēṇṭum eṉṟārkaḷ. ñāṉāmpāḷ avarkaḷi nōkki nīṅkaḷ uttēcittirukkiṟa kāriyam puktamāyum cāttiyamāyum irukkiṟa pakṣattil antappaṭi ceyyat taṭiyilli. āṉāl kāriyam iṉṉateṉṟu terintukoḷḷāmal, munti vākkuttattam ceyvatu cariyallavē eṉṟāḷ. antap pirapukkaḷ ñāṉāmpāḷip pārttu nīṅkaḷ kaliyāṇamillāmal piramacāriyā yiruppatu eṅkaḷukkup periya maṉōviyākūlamā yirukkiṟatu. takka paruvattil kaliyāṇañ ceyyātiruppatāl uṅkaḷuṭiya tēkam nāḷukku nāḷ iḷittup pōkiṉṟatu. nīṅkaḷ cukamāyiruntāl tāṉē eṅkaḷukkuc cukam uṇṭu. nāṅkaḷ ettaṉiyō upakāraṅkaḷip peṟṟuk koṇṭōm. uṅkaḷukku nāṅkaḷ eṉṉa upakārañ ceyyap pōkiṟōm? uṅkaḷuṭiya kaliyāṇa makōṟcavattip pārkka vēṇṭumeṉṟu eṅkaḷ kaṇkaḷ apēkṣikkiṉṟaṉa. eṅkaḷuṭiya muntiṉa rājāviṉ makaḷuṭiya aḻakuṅ kuṇamum uṅkaḷukku naṉṟāyt teriyum. antap peṇṇiṉuṭiya pāṭṭiyār mutalāṉa pantukkaḷuṭiya karuttiyum aṟintōm. avarkaḷ ellārum antap peṇṇi uṅkaḷukkuk kaṉṉikātāṉañ ceyyap pūraṇa cammatamāyirukkiṟārkaḷ. antap peṇṇiṉuṭiya karuttun taṅkaḷiyē nāṭiyiruppatākavun terintu koṇṭōm. uṅkaḷiyum antap peṇṇiyum atirṣṭam vantu maṭiyip piṭittu iḻukkumpōtu nīṅkaḷ vēṇṭām eṉpīrkaḷā? antak kaṉṉikārettiṉattukkut takka māppiḷḷi nīṅkaḷē! uṅkaḷukkut takka peṇ antap peṇṇēyaṉṟi vēṟilli eṉṟārkaḷ. itik kēṭṭavuṭaṉē ñāṉāmpāl tiṭukkiṭṭut tikittu oṉṟum pēcāmal eṉ mukattip pārttāḷ. avaḷ poy pēcukiṟavaḷāyiruntāl, camayāṉukūlamāka eppaṭiyāvatu poy collit tappittukkoḷḷuvāḷ. poyyuñ collak kūṭāmal meyyuñ collakkūṭāmal iruntapaṭiyāl avaḷ ivvāṟu malippataṟku iṭamāyiṟṟu. nāṉ antap pirapukkaḷip pārttu nīṅkaḷ colluvatu periya kāriyamāṉatāl uṭaṉē eppaṭi maṟumoḻi collakkūṭum? eṉṟēṉ. avarkaḷ eṉṉip pārttu uparājap pirapuvē! inta viṣayattil ālōcikkavēṇṭiya caṅkati eṉṉa irukkiṟatu? peṇṇiṉuṭiya kuṇattip paṟṟi vicārikkap pōkiṟīrkaḷā? allatu kulattip paṟṟi vicārikkap pōkiṟīrkaḷā? ellā viṣayamum uṅkaḷukkut terintatu tāṉē. iṉṟikku maṟumoḻi collak kūṭāviṭṭālum, nāḷikkāvatu eṅkaḷuṭiya iṣṭappaṭi maṟumoḻi colla vēṇṭum. uparājāvākiya nīṅkaḷ anta makārājāvukkuc colli akattiyam eṅkaḷ iṣṭappaṭi niṟivēṟṟavēṇṭum. anta rāja kaṉṉiyiṉ maṉam makārājāvi nāṭiyiruppatāl makārājā antap peṇṇi vēṇṭāmeṉṟāl peṇpāvam allavā? ākiyāl nīṅkaḷum muyaṟci ceytu intak kāriyatti niṟivēṟṟiṉāl uṅkaḷukkum vēṟē peṇ tēṭi vivākañ ceyvikkiṟōm. anukūlamāṉa maṟumoḻiyik kēṭpataṟkāka nāḷikku āvaluṭaṉē varuvōm eṉṟu colliviṭṭu ellārum pōyviṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ pōṉa vakiyip pārttāl taṅkaḷuṭiya vārttiyi nāṅkaḷ arttāṅkīkāram paṇṇikkoṇṭatāka niṉittup pōṉatākat tōṉṟiṟṟu. eṉakkum peṇ tēṭi vivākañ ceyvatāka avarkaḷ coṉṉavuṭaṉē tuyara mukamāyirunta ñāṉāmpāḷukkum puṉṉaki uṇṭākip piṟaku aṭakkik koṇṭāḷ.

avarkaḷ ellārum pōṉapiṟaku ñāṉāmpāḷ eṉṉip pārttu tukka mukattuṭaṉē collukiṟāḷ:- viḷiyāṭṭuc caṇṭi viṉic caṇṭiyāṉatu pōla nāṉ āṇvēṣam pūṇṭukoṇṭu pēṭicam paṇṇiṉatu ivvaḷavu piramātamāy viḷintirukkiṟatu. nāṉ ātiyilē uṇmiyic colliyiruntāl ivvaḷavu viparītam nēriṭumā? eṉṉuṭiya āṇ vēṣatti ettaṉi naparkaḷ mōcam pōkiṟārkaḷ? mukkiyamāka anta rājaputtiriyiṉuṭiya nilimi mikavum paritāpattiṟkuriyatāka irukkiṟatu. antap pirapukkaḷ colvatu vāstavamā yirukkiṟa pakṣattil avaḷi nāṉ koḷḷamāṭṭēṉeṉṟu nirākarippatāka kēḷvippaṭumpōtu avaḷukku ettaṉi tukkattiṟku iṭamāyirukkum? tāy takappaṉ illāta oru arumiyāṉa peṇṇi, nām ivvakiyāka tuṉpappaṭuttuvatu takumā? atu peṇ turōkam allavā? ākiyāl kāriyam iṉṉum piramātamāy vaḷaruvataṟku muṉ uṇmiyic colliviṭuvatu uttamamākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. nāḷittiṉam avarkaḷ vantu kēṭkumpōtu nāṉ peṇṇeṉpati avarkaḷukkut terivikka yōcittirukkiṟēṉ eṉṟāḷ. nāṉ avaḷip pārttu nī colvatellām vāstavantāṉ. āṉāl ippōtu uṇmiyi veḷiviṭuvatu vivēkamāyt tōṉṟavilli. jaṉaṅkaḷ ellārum uṉṉi puruṣaṉeṉṟē niṉittuc cakala upacāra mariyātikaḷum vaṇakkamuñ ceytu varukiṟārkaḷ. nī peṇṇeṉṟu tiṭīreṉṟu kēḷvippaṭṭa māttirattil avarkaḷuṭiya putti eppaṭiyirukkumō teriyātu. intat tēcattār stirīkaḷi nitiruṣṭamāka eṇṇi avarkaḷip paṭṭāpiṣēkattukku yōkkiyarkaḷ allaveṉṟu niṉikkiṟārkaḷ. aṉṟiyum nām anēka uttiyōkastarkaḷiyum paṭikaḷiyum nīkki anēka ruṭiya virōtaṅkaḷic campātittuk koṇṭirukkiṟōm. ākiyāl intac camayattil uṇmiyic colvatu cariyallaveṉṟu niṉikkiṟēṉ eṉṟēṉ. uṭaṉē avaḷ nāṉ uṇmikkākat tali koṭukkac cittamāyirukkiṟēṉ. āṉāl oru kāriyattip paṟṟi maṭṭum yōcikkiṟēṉ eṉṟu colli antak kāriyam iṉṉateṉṟu terivikkāmal muṉamāyiruntāḷ. avaḷ kuṟittuc coṉṉa kāriyam iṉṉateṉṟu eṉakku naṉṟāka viḷaṅkiṟṟu. avaḷ uṇmikkākat taṉ tali koṭukkac cittamāyiruntālum eṉakku eṉṉa pollāṅku viḷiyumōveṉṟu maṭṭum ālōcippatākat terintu koṇṭēṉ. ñāṉāmpāḷ eṉṉiṭattil vittirukkiṟa aṇikaṭanta aṉpiṉ nimittam avaḷ pala caṅkaṭaṅkaḷukku uṭpaṭṭiruppati niṉikkumpōtu eṉ maṉam patittu uli meḻuku pōl urukiṟṟu. yātoru apāyamumillāmal avaḷi rakṣikkavēṇṭumeṉṟu aṭikkaṭi nāṉ kaṭavuḷip pirārttittuk koṇṭēṉ.

maṟunāṭ kāliyilum mantiri piratāṉi mutaliya cakala uttiyōkastarkaḷum ūriluḷḷa cakala pirapukkaḷum araṇmaṉiyil vantu kūṭṭaṅ kūṭiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ñāṉāmpāḷik kaṇṭavuṭaṉē “makārājāvē! nāṅkaḷ niṉitta kāriyam niṟivēṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟu kōyilukkuk kōyil pirārttaṉi ceytu koṇṭirukkiṟōm. eṅkaḷuṭiya kulateyvam intak kāriyatti akattiyaṅ kūṭṭi muṭikkumeṉṟu niccayamāy nampiyirukkiṟōm. ataṟkut tiruṣṭāntamāka nāṅkaḷ vaḻiyil varumpōtu nalla nalla cakuṉaṅkaḷ kaṇṭōm. intak kaliyāṇam muṭiyavēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpātavarkaḷ oruvarumilli. ariñāṇ kaṭṭiṉa piḷḷikaḷ mutalākac cakalarum āvalākak kāttukkoṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. ittaṉi jaṉaṅkaḷuṭiya eṇṇattukku virōtañ ceyya māṭṭīrkaḷeṉṟu nampukiṟōm” eṉṟārkaḷ.

ñāṉāmpāḷ avarkaḷi nōkki “ittaṉi jaṉaṅkaḷuṭiya apēṣikku virōtam ceyvatu eṉakku meyyākavē varuttamāyirukkiṟatu. āṉāl eṉṉālē kūṭāta kāriyattukku nāṉ eṉṉa ceyvēṉ? antap peṇṇi nāṉ koḷvataṟku oru periya piratipantamirukkiṟatu. atu oruvarālum nivartti ceyyak kūṭātatāyirukkiṟatu” eṉṟāḷ.

jaṉaṅkaḷ “antap piratipantam iṉṉateṉṟu terivittāl eṅkaḷuṭiya pirāṇaṉik koṭuttāyiṉum allatu eṅkaḷuṭiya āstikaḷiyellāñ celavaḻittāyiṉum antap piratipantatti nivartti ceykiṟōm” eṉṟārkaḷ.

ñāṉāmpāḷ ”antap piratipantatti nivartti ceyya oruvarāluṅ kūṭātu. peṇṇi āṇākkavum āṇip peṇṇākkavum eppaṭikkūṭātō appaṭiyē ituvuṅ kūṭāta kāriyantāṉ. aṉṟiyum nīṅkaḷ kaliyāṇamillāta piramacāriyeṉṟu niṉittu eṉakkuk kaliyāṇam ceyvikka muyalukiṟīrkaḷ. nāṉ piramacāriyalla” eṉṟāḷ.

jaṉaṅkaḷ ”uṅkaḷukku muṉṉamē kaliyāṇam naṭantiruntāluṅ kūṭa anta rājakaṉṉiyi uṅkaḷukkut tutiya vivākañ ceyya ellārum cammatikkiṟārkaḷ. cakala caṅkatikaḷiyum nāṅkaḷ muṉṉamē kalantu pēcikkoṇṭu tāṉ uṅkaḷiṭattil vantōm. oru peṇjāti yirukkumpōtu vēṟu stirīkaḷi vivākañ ceyvatu cāstirōktamē tavira acāstiriyam allavē eṉṟārkaḷ.

ñāṉāmpāḷ “uṅkaḷuṭiya apippirāyappaṭi pala stirī vivākam cāstirōktamāka yiruntālum koḷḷukiṟavaṉuṭiya cammatam vēṇṭāmā?” eṉṟāḷ.

jaṉaṅkaḷ ”koḷḷukiṟavaṉuṭiya cammatattik kēḷāmalē, intat tēcattil kaliyāṇaṅkaḷ naṭappatu vaḻakkamāyirukkiṉṟatu. nāṅkaḷ appaṭic ceyyat tuṇiyāmal uṅkaḷuṭiya cammatatti kēṭkavē vantirukkiṟōm. ittaṉi jaṉaṅkaḷuṭiya pirārttaṉiyi nirākarippatu niyāyamā? eṅkaḷi pārāviṭṭālum tāytakappaṉ illāta antap peṇ mukattiyāvatu pāruṅkaḷ! antap peṇ uṅkaḷi vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpumpōtu nīṅkaḷ vēṇṭāmeṉṟu taḷḷiviṭuvatu peṇ turōkam allavā?” eṉṟārkaḷ.

ñāṉāmpāḷ “antap peṇṇukkāka ivvaḷavu parintu pēcukiṟa nīṅkaḷ antap peṇṇukkuc ceyya vēṇṭiya oru mukkiyamāṉa naṉmiyic ceyyāmalirukkiṟīrkaḷ. anta naṉmiyic ceytāl rājarājākkaḷellōrum antap peṇṇi virumpuvārkaḷē!“ eṉṟāḷ.

jaṉaṅkaḷ “antap peṇṇukku nāṅkaḷ eṉṉa naṉmi ceyyāmal viṭṭuviṭṭōm? atu iṉṉateṉṟu uttiravāṉāl inta nimiṣattil ceykiṟōm. itu cattiyam cattiyam“ eṉṟārkaḷ.

ñāṉāmpāḷ “antak kaṉṉikiyiṉuṭiya takappaṉār puruṣa cantatiyillāmal iṟantupōṉa uṭaṉē pārāmpari pāttiyak kiramappaṭi antap peṇṇukku paṭṭāpiṣēkam ceyyavēṇṭiyatu niyāyamāyirukka, nīṅkaḷ antappaṭi ceyyat tappip pōyviṭṭīrkaḷ! oru rājā iṟanta uṭaṉē pārāmpari pāttiyakiramatti aṉucarikkāmal putu rājāvi niyamikkiṟatāyiruntāl periya kalakaṅkaḷukku āspatamākum. eppaṭiyeṉṟāl palavāṉāyirukkiṟa ovvoruvaṉun taṉṉi niyamikkavēṇṭum taṉṉi niyamikkavēṇṭumeṉṟum vallaṭi vaḻakkuc ceyvāṉ. ovvoruvaṉuṭiya kaṭciyilum palapēr cērntu yuttan toṭaṅki oruvariyoruvar māyttuk koḷvārkaḷ. niyāya paripālaṉam naṭavāmal niṉṟu pōkumāṉatāl ūrilē tiruṭṭukaḷum puraṭṭukaḷum koḷḷikaḷum kolikaḷum atikarikkum. itutāṉ camayameṉṟu anniya tēcattu catrukkaḷum piravēcittuc carva koḷḷiyaṭippārkaḷ. oru rājā iṟantavuṭaṉē avaṉuṭiya cantati āṇāyiruntālum peṇṇāyiruntālum antac cantatikkē paṭṭamākiṟapakṣattil oru kalakattukkum iṭamilli. atu carvajaṉa cammatamāyirukkum. rājāviṉuṭiya puttiraṉ allatu puttiriki viṣayattil cakala jaṉaṅkaḷukkum atika keḷaravamum matippum payapaktiyum uṇṭākum. yurājāvukku cāvilliru eṉkiṟa nīti vākkiyappaṭi oru aracaṉ enta nimiṣattil iṟantu pōkiṟāṉō anta nimiṣattil avaṉuṭiya vāricukkut tēcātipattiyam uṇṭākiṟapaṭiyāl, niyāyaparipālaṉaṅkaḷellām nillāmal toṭarcciyāy naṭantuvarum. āṇ cantatiyāvatu peṇ cantatiyāvatu allatu vēṟē urimikkāraṉāvatu illāmal oru aracaṉ māṇṭupōkiṟa pakṣattil, putu aracaṉ niyamikkalāmē yallātu, takunta cutantaravāḷikaḷirukkumpōtu putu aracaṉi niyamippatu acaṅkatam. cakala viṣayaṅkaḷum makā mantirālōcaṉic capiyāruṭiya aṉumatippaṭi naṭakkiṟapaṭiyāl rājāviṉ vāricukaḷ tiṟamiyaṟṟavarkaḷāyiruntālumkūṭa avarkaḷi niyamikkat taṭiyilli. rājāviṉuṭiya vārcukaḷi niyamippatiṉāl uṇṭākiṟa naṉmiyiyum ataṉāl viḷiyattakka tīṅkiyum cīrtūkkip pārkkumiṭattil tīmiyip pārkkilum naṉmikaḷ atikamāyirukkiṟapaṭiyāl pārampari kiramappaṭi niyamippatē ucitamāyirukkiṟatu. appaṭiyē putu aracaṉiyāvatu allatu kuṭiyaraciyāvatu niyamippataṉāl uṇṭākiṟa cātaka pātakaṅkaḷi yōcikkumiṭattil cātakattip pārkkilum pātakam periyatāyiruppatāl puturāja niyamaṉattiyuṅ kuṭiyaraciyum niṣētikkavēṇṭiyatu, ellāruṭiya kaṭamiyākavum irukkiṟatu. yāṉiyiṉuṭiya kiyilē pūmāliyik koṭuttu yār kaḻuttilē pōṭukiṟatō avaṉi aracaṉāka niyamippatu inta ūr vaḻakkamāyirukkiṟtu. maṉuṣarkaḷuṭiya yōkkiyati yāṉikku eppaṭit teriyakkūṭum? atu oru mūṭaṉ kaḻuttilē māliyip pōṭṭālum avaṉi aracaṉāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷavēṇṭiyatutāṉē! atip pārkkilum iṟantu pōṉa rājāviṉuṭiya pāttiyastarkaḷiyē niyamippatu carva cilākkiyam allavā? uṅkaḷuṭiya atirṣṭa vacattāl uṅkaḷuṭiya paḻiya rājāviṉ kumāratti paṭṭāpiṣēkattiṟkuc cakala vitattilum yōkkiyati uḷḷavaḷākayirukkiṟāḷ. antap peṇṇukku makuṭañ cūṭṭa nīṅkaḷ oru ākṣēpamuñ collamāṭṭīrkaḷeṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ eṉṟāḷ.

jaṉaṅkaḷ makārājāvē! uṅkaḷip pōla tarmarājākkaḷ inta pūmaṇṭalattil iruppārkaḷā? taṅkaḷuṭiya paṭṭatti vēṟoruvarukkuk koṭukka yārāvatu cammatippārkaḷā? takappaṉ jīvantaṉāyirukkumpōtē taṉ piḷiḷikkup paṭṭāpiṣēkañ ceyyac cammatikkiṟatilli. ippōtu rarajākkaḷ oru coṟpa tēcattic campātippataṟkāka ettaṉi uyirkaḷik koṉṟu evvaḷavō pāṭupaṭukiṟārkaḷ. purāṇaṅkaḷilē collappaṭṭa irāmaṉ, tarmaṉ, ariccantiraṉ, naḷaṉ mutaliya aracarkaḷ kūṭa nirppantattiṉāl cila nāḷ rājāṅkatti viṭṭu nīṅkiyiruntārkaḷē yallātu maṉappūrvamāy viṭṭavarkaḷ oruvarumilli. anta aracarkaḷ ellārum uṅkaḷukkuc camāṉamāvārkaḷā? ippaṭippaṭṭa tarma rājāvi nāṅkaḷ orukālattilum viṭuvōmā? eṅkaḷuṭiya apīṣṭappaṭikkum uṅkaḷuṭiya maṉōpīṣṭappaṭikkum, anta rāja kaṉṉikikku makuṭamum māliyuñ cūṭṭi, nīṅkaḷ mūvarum kūṭi aracu purivatik kāṇa virumpukiṟōm eṉṟu colli carva jaṉaṅkaḷum viṭi peṟṟukkoṇṭu pōyviṭṭārkaḷ.





45-ām atikāram

irājāṅka parittiyākam—tāy tantiyaric

cantittal—ānanta valliyiṉ makuṭāpiṣēkam



jaṉaṅkaḷ ellārum pōṉapiṉpu, ñāṉāmpāḷ eṉṉip pārttu “attāṉ! anta jaṉaṅkaḷ colvatip pārttāl viparītamāyirukkiṟatu. avarkaḷ nammuṭiya iṣṭappaṭi antap peṇṇukkup paṭṭāpiṣēkañ ceyyac cammatittatu cantōṣamāṉa kāriyantāṉ. āṉāl antap peṇṇukku nāṉ māli cūṭṭi, rōm paṭṭaṇatti oru kālattilē mūvēntarkaḷ āṇṭatu pōla nīṅkaḷum nāṉum antap peṇṇum ākiya mūvaruṅkūṭi aracāḷavēṇṭumeṉpatu jaṉaṅkaḷuṭiya tāṟpariyam pōlak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. intat tarmacaṅkaṭattukku eṉṉa ceykiṟatu?“ eṉṟāḷ. nāṉ ñāṉāmpāḷip pārttu “nī cammatittuttāṉē antap peṇṇukku māli cūṭṭa vēṇṭum? uṉṉuṭiya cammatamillāmal yār eṉṉa ceyyakkūṭum? antap peṇṇukkuc cīkkirattil makuṭāpiṣēkañ ceytuviṭṭu nām oruvarukkun teriyāmal inta ūri vīṭṭup puṟappaṭṭuk kampi nīṭṭiviṭṭāl appāl jaṉaṅkaḷ eṉṉa ceyvārkaḷ? ākiyāl nī oṉṟukkum añca vēṇṭām“ eṉṟu avaḷukkut tēṟutal coṉṉēṉ.

aṉṟiyat tiṉam cāyarakṣi vītikku vīti pēriki muḻakkamum jaṉaṅkaḷuṭiya cantōṣa āravāramuṅ kēṭṭu “atu eṉṉa captam?” eṉṟu cāraṇarkaḷi vicārittōm. avarkaḷ eṅkaḷip pārttu “makārājāvukkum paḻiya aracaruṭiya puttirikkum varukiṟa cukkiravāraṅ kāliyil' kaliyāṇa mukūrttamum aṉṟiyattiṉañ cāyaṅkālam anta rājaputtirikkup paṭṭāpiṣēkamum naṭappatākavum ataṟkāka ellārum ūri alaṅkarikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu muracu muḻakkukiṟārkaḷ. atik kēṭṭu jaṉaṅkaḷ ellārum ānanta kōṣam ceykiṟārkaḷ” eṉṟārkaḷ.itik kēṭṭavuṭaṉē nāṅkaḷ piramittuc ciṟitu nēram cili pōla aciyāmal uṭkārntiruntōm. antac camayattil mantirikaḷ vantu nuḻintārkaḷ. ñāṉāmpāḷ avarkaḷip pārttu muracu aṟiyumpaṭi yār uttaravu koṭuttārkaḷ? eṉṟu viṉaviṉāḷ. mantirikaḷ makārājāvē! iṉṟu kāliyil nīṅkaḷ coṉṉa apippirāyattukku virōtamāka oṉṟum naṭakkavilli. paṭṭāpiṣēkamuṅ kaliyāṇamum orē tiṉattil naṭakkavēṇṭumeṉpatu ellāruṭiya pirārttaṉiyākavum irukkiṟatu. inta viṣayattil ellārum oru maṉamāyum orē kumukkamāyumirukkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭiya karuttukku virōtañ ceytāl peruṅ kalakattukku iṭamākumeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. mēlum antak kumāratti taṉakkup paṭṭāpiṣēkañ ceytuviṭṭu nīṅkaḷ pōyviṭuvīrkaḷeṉṟu niṉittu taṉakkup paṭṭāpiṣēkamē vēṇṭāmeṉṟu aḻutatu. nīṅkaḷ taṉṉik kaliyāṇañ ceytukoḷvīrkaḷeṉṟu kēḷvippaṭṭa piṟaku tāṉ atu cantōṣamāyirukkiṟatu. uṅkaḷuṭiya muyaṟciyiṉālē taṉakkup paṭṭāpiṣēkam ākiṟateṉṟu terintu koṇṭu muṉṉiyip pārkkilum nuṟu paṅku atikamāka uṅkaḷiṭattiṟ pakṣamum pācamumāyirukkiṟatu. ākiyāl antap peṇṇiṉuṭiya āciyik keṭukka vēṇṭām, makārājāvē! eṉṟu colli nāṅkaḷ maṟumoḻi colvataṟku iṭamillāmal tiṭīreṉṟu caṭutiyiṟ pōyviṭṭārkaḷ. intac camāccāraṅkaḷik kēṭṭapiṉpu, muṉṉē eṉakkirunta tiriyam nīṅki eṉpāṭun taṭumāṟṟattil vantuviṭṭatu. “ēṟac coṉṉāl erutukkuk kōpam, iṟaṅkac coṉṉāl noṇṭikkuk kōpam“ eṉpatu pōla, nāṅkaḷ anta ūril iruntāl ñāṉāmpāḷ antap peṇṇukku akattiyam tāli kaṭṭa vēṇṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. māṭṭē ṉeṉṟāl, ūrāruṭiya pakiyiyum, antap peṇ paḻiyiyum, campātittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. eṅkaḷuṭiya ūrukkup pōkalāmeṉṟāl, kaṭalum malikaḷuñ cūḻnta vikkiramapuriyi viṭṭu iṉṉa mārkkamāyp pōkiṟateṉṟu teriyavilli. araṇmaṉiyil eṅkaḷic cūḻntirukkiṟavarkaḷ ellārum maṇamuracu kēṭṭuc cantōṣikkiṟavarkaḷā yiruntatāl nāṉum ñāṉāmpāḷum cakala caṅkatikaḷiyum kalantu tārāḷamāyp pēcavum cantarppam illāmal pōy viṭṭatu.

oruvariyuṅ kūṭa aḻittukkoṇṭu pōkāmal nāṉum ñāṉāmpāḷum viṭivataṟkumuṉ eḻuntu cila camayaṅkaḷil veḷiyē ulāvap pōkiṟatu vaḻakkamāyiruntatu. nāṅkaḷ ātiyil vikkiramapurikku vantapōtu ēṟi vanta maliyiṉmēl ēṟi ulāvuvatum, anta malimēlirukkiṟa araṇmaṉiyil iraṇṭoru nāḷ vacippatum vaḻakkamāyiruntatu. anta vaḻakkappaṭi pōkiṟavarkaḷ pōl nāṉum ñāṉāmpāḷum oru nāḷ naṭuccāmattil eḻuntu vēṟoruvariyuṅ kūṭa aḻittukkoṇṭu pōkāmal nāṅkaḷ maṭṭum puṟappaṭṭup pōy anta malimēl ēṟiṉōm. ēṟiṉa uṭaṉē nāṉ ñāṉāmpāḷip pārttu “iṉimēl inta ūril iruppatu cariyalla; āṉāl nammuṭiya ūrukkāvatu ātiyūrukkāvatu enta mārkkamāyp pōkiṟateṉṟu teriyavilli. nām muṉṉē vanta vaḻiyāyp pōkalā meṉṟāl, tuṣṭa mirukaṅkaḷ niṟinta kāṭukaḷit tāṇṭi eppaṭip pōkakkūṭum?“ eṉṟēṉ. ñāṉāmpāḷ eṉṉip pārttu “inta ūriliruntu peṇṇum peṇṇuṅ kaliyāṇañ ceytukoḷvatip pārkkilum anta mirukaṅkaḷuṭaṉ vācam ceyvatu culapamāyt tōṉṟukiṟatu“ eṉṟāḷ. avaḷ maṟupaṭiyum eṉṉip pārttu “nām muṉṉēṟivanta maliyiṉ teṉpuṟattu vaḻiyāka iṟaṅkip pārppōm. kaṭavuḷuṭiya kirupiyāl antak kāṭukaḷik kaṭantu pōvataṟkut takka mārkkaṅ kiṭittālum kiṭikkum” eṉṟāḷ. antap pirakāram teṉpuṟattil iṟaṅki, aruṇōtayattukku aṭivārattil vantu cērntōm. aṭivārattil muṉṉē irunta kāṭukaḷellām aḻikkappaṭṭup pōy, maṉuṣarkaḷ cañcarikkumpaṭiyāyiruntatu. uṭaṉē nāṅkaḷ svāmikku naṉṟiyaṟinta stōttirañ ceytukoṇṭu teṟku vaḻiyāka kaṭu naṭiyāka naṭantu pōṉōm. ñāṉāmpāḷ āṇ vēṣatti māṟṟip peṇ vaṭivākavē vantāḷ. oru periya rājāṅkattiyum tivviya cuntaramāṉa oru rāja puttiriyiyum vēṇṭāmeṉṟu viṭṭuviṭṭu ōṭukiṟavarkaḷ eṅkaḷit tavira vēṟoruvarum irukkamāṭṭārkaḷ.

payam piṉṉē yiruntu eṅkaḷip piṭittut taḷḷikkoṇṭu pōṉatāl, ōṭukiṟa ciramaṅkūṭat teriyāmal kāta vaḻi tūram ōṭiṉōm. piṟaku koñca tūrattil oru periya kūṭāram aṭikkappaṭṭiruntatu. yārō vantu kūṭāram aṭittirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu nāṅkaḷ pēcikkoṇṭu antak kūṭārattukku nērē pōy uḷḷē nuḻintōm. tēvarājap piḷḷiyuṅ kaṉakacapiyum uḷḷēyiruntavarkaḷ eṅkaḷik kaṇṭavuṭaṉē āvaluṭaṉ ōṭi vantu taḻuvikkoṇṭu, caṟṟu nēram aṅkalāyttup piṟaku kaḷikūrntārkaḷ. nāṉ tēvarājappiḷḷiyiyup pārttu aiyā! nāṅkaḷ periya āpattukkut tappi ōṭivantirukkiṟōm. cilavici yārāvatu eṅkaḷit toṭarntu vantālum varuvārkaḷ. ākiyāl nām puṟappaṭṭu ātiyūrukkup pōvatu naṉmi. vaḻiyiṟ pōkumpōtu cakala camācāraṅkaḷum collukiṟēṉ eṉṟēṉ. uṭaṉē iraṇṭu vaṇṭikaḷiṟ kutirikaḷ pūṭṭi, oru vaṇṭiyil ñāṉāmpāḷi ēṟṟuvittukkoṇṭu, maṟṟoru vaṇṭiyil nāṉum tēvarājap piḷḷiyum kaṉakacapiyum ēṟikkoṇṭu puṟappaṭṭōm. eṉṉiyum ñāṉāmpāḷiyum paṭṭattu yāṉi tūkkikkoṇṭu pōy maliyil viṭṭatum, nāṅkaḷ vikkiramapurikkup pōy aracāṇṭatum mutaliya cakala caṅkatikaḷiyum nāṉ vaḻiyil varumpōtē, tēvarājap piḷḷikkum kaṉakacapikkum terivittēṉ. ñāṉāmpāḷ puruṣa vēṣam pūṇṭukoṇṭu aracāṇṭa vakiyik kēḷvippaṭṭu, avarkaḷ mitamillāta āccariyamum ānantamum aṭintārkaḷ. ñāṉāmpāḷip puruṣaṉeṉṟu niṉittu anta rājakaṉṉikiyākiya ānantavalli iccip paṭṭatum avarkaḷ iruvarukkum vivākam ceyvataṟkāka mukūrttaṅkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭatum, ataṟkākat tappi nāṅkaḷ ōṭi vantatum kēḷvippaṭṭut takappaṉum piḷḷiyum viḻuntu viḻuntu cirittārkaḷ. poḻutu viṭiyavum viṭiyāmalirukkavum nāṉ pāṭiṉatum kēḷvippaṭṭavuṭaṉē avarkaḷ āṭiyāṭikkoṇṭu ciritta cirippiṉāl nāṅkaḷ ēṟiyirunta paṇṭi kalakalattup pōy viṭṭatu.

avarkaḷuṭiya cirippu aṭaṅkiṉa piṟaku, eṉṉuṭiya tāy tantiyar māmaṉār māmiyār mutaliyavarkaḷip paṟṟi vicārittēṉ. tēvarājap piḷḷi eṉṉip pārttuc collukiṟār:- nīṅkaḷ iruvarum kāṇāmaṟpōṉa camācāratti uṭaṉē avarkaḷukkut terivittēṉ. avarkaḷ cakikkakkūṭāta tukkam uṭiyavarkaḷāy ātiyūrukku vantārkaḷ. intak kāṭṭu vaḻiyāka anta yāṉi uṅkaḷiruvariyun taṉittaṉiyē tūkkikkoṇṭu pōṉatip pārttatākac cilar coṉṉapaṭiyāl uṅkaḷit tēṭuvataṟkāka intak kāṭukaḷiyellām aḻittu nirmūlañ ceytatumiṉṟi mirukaṅkaḷiyum koṉṟu nācañ ceytuviṭṭōm. ippōtu nāṅkaḷ malikaḷiyum araṇiyaṅkaḷiyum aṇuaṇuvāy ārāyntu pārttōm. iṉṉamum uṅkaḷit tēṭip pārppataṟkākavē naṭukkāṭṭil kūṭāram aṭittukkoṇṭu ivviṭattil vācamāyiruntōm. uṅkaḷ tāy tantiyar, māmaṉār, māmiyār mutalāṉavarkaḷum eṅkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭa vantiruntu uṅkaḷit tēṭip pārttum nīṅkaḷ akappaṭavilli. avarkaḷ uṅkaḷip pirinta ēkkattiṉāl cariyāṉa ūṇ uṟakkamillāmal turumpu pōl iḷittup pōyiruppatāl avarkaḷi intak kāṭṭilirukka vēṇṭāmeṉṟu kaṭṭāyap paṭutti ātiyūrukku aṉuppiviṭṭōm. avarkaḷi nīṅkaḷ pārttāl aṭiyāḷaṅ kaṇṭupiṭikka māṭṭīrkaḷ. iṉṉum oru mācam avarkaḷ uṅkaḷik kāṇāmaliruppārkaḷāṉāl avarkaḷ jīvittiruppatu pirayācam. eṅkaḷ ūril uṅkaḷi niṟuttikkoṇṭa nimittam ippaṭippaṭṭa viyācaṅkaḷ uṅkaḷukku nēriṭṭapaṭiyāl nāṅkaḷ paṭṭa cañcalam koñcam alla. kaṭavuḷ eṅkaḷuṭiya pirārttaṉikku iraṅki maṟupaṭiyum uṅkaḷi kṣēmamāyk koṇṭu cērttataṟkāka avarukku nāṅkaḷ attiyanta kirutakñarkaḷā yirukkiṟōm eṉṟār. eṉṉuṭiya tanti tāy mutalāṉavarkaḷ ātiyūril vantiruppatākak kēḷvippaṭṭavuṭaṉē avarkaḷic cīkkirattiṟ pārkka vēṇṭumeṉkiṟa periya avāvuṭaṉ ceṉṟēṉ.

muṉṉē eṅkaḷi yāṉi tūkkikkoṇṭu pōṉa vaḻi cuṟṟuvaḻiyākavum ippōtu nāṅkaḷ pōvatu nēr vaḻiyākavum iruntapaṭiyāl nāṅkal puṟappaṭṭa nālām nāḷ cāyarakṣi ātiyūri aṭintōm. eṉṉiyum ñāṉāmpāḷiyum pārttavuṭaṉē eṅkaḷuṭiya tāy tantikaḷ ōṭivantu eṅkaḷi oruvar māṟṟi oruvar kaṭṭikkoṇṭu piralāpittup piṟaku maṉantēṟiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭiya tēkameliviyum iḷippiyum pārttavuṭaṉē eṅkaḷukku āṟṟāmiyum tukkamum uṇṭāki iṉi mēl oru kālattilum avarkaḷi viṭṭup pirikiṟatilliyeṉṟu caṅkētañ ceytukoṇṭōm. nāṅkaḷ ūri viṭṭup pōṉapiṟaku vikkiramapuriyil nikaḻnta cakala varttamāṉaṅkaḷiyum eṉṉiṭattil kēḷvippaṭṭa pirakāram tēvarājap piḷḷiyum kaṉakacapiyum varṇittu varṇittu eṉ tāy tantiyar, māmaṉār māmiyār mutalāṉavarkaḷukkut teriyappaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. atik kēṭṭavuṭaṉē ellārukkum uṇṭāṉa āccariyamum āṉantamum aparimitamē. tēvarājap piḷḷiyuṅ kaṉakacapiyuñ coṉṉatu pōtāteṉṟu anta aticayaṅkaḷi yellām eṉ vāyālē orutaram kēṭṭārkaḷ. piṟaku nāṉ coṉṉatu pōtāteṉṟu ñāṉāmpāl vāyālē oru taram kēṭṭārkaḷ. ivvakiyāka anta iravu muḻuvatum tēvarājappiḷḷi vīṭu cantōṣa amarkkaḷamāyiruntatē yaṉṟi oruvarāvatu uṟaṅkavilli.

maṟunāṭ kāliyil ñāṉāmpāḷ eṉṉiṭattilum eṉ tāyāriṭattilum ālōcaṉi ceytukoṇṭu tāṉ peṇṇeṉpatu mutalāṉa vivaraṅkaḷik kāṭṭi aṭiyiṟ kaṇṭapaṭi ānantavallikku oru nirupam eḻutiyaṉuppiṉāḷ:-

eṉ piriyamāṉa taṅkiyē!

makārājāvāka vikkiramapuriyi aracāṇṭa nāṉ uṉṉippōṟ peṇṇē tavira āṇ alla. uparājāvāyiruntu anta ūri āṇṭavar tāṉ eṉṉuṭiya kaṇavar. nāṉ āṇvēṣam pūṇṭukoṇṭu eṉṉiviṭṭup pirintu pōṉa eṉ pirāṇa nāyakarit tēṭikkoṇṭu vanta iṭattil eṉakku rājapaṭṭaṅ kiṭittu nāṉ aracāṇṭa vivaraṅkaḷellām uṉakkut teriyumē! eṉṉip puruṣaṉeṉṟu niṉittu eṉṉi nī pāṇikkirakaṇañ ceytukoḷḷa virumpiyatāka nāṉ kēḷvippaṭṭu aḷavaṟṟa viyākūlam aṭintēṉ. pala kāraṇaṅkaḷāl nāṉ peṇṇeṉkiṟa uṇmiyi uṉakkut terivikkak kūṭāmaṟ pōyviṭṭatu. uṉakkup paṭṭāpiṣēkañ ceytuviṭṭu nāṉ veḷiyē vantuviṭalāmeṉṟu niṉittu jaṉaṅ kaḷiṭattil uṉṉuṭiya paṭṭāpiṣēkattik kuṟittup pēciṉēṉ. avarkaḷum eṉṉuṭiya vārttiyi aṅkīkarittuk koṇṭārkaḷ. piṟaku uṉakkum eṉakkum vivākamum paṭṭāpiṣēkamum orē tiṉattil naṭappatāka eṉṉuṭiya anumatiyillāmal jaṉaṅkaḷ pēriki muḻaṅkip piracittañ ceytapaṭiyāl iṉi mēl avviṭattil iruppatu cariyallaveṉṟu niṉittu nāṉum eṉ nāyakarum veḷippaṭṭu vantu viṭṭōm. uṉakkum maṟṟavarkaḷukkum uṇmi teriyātāṉapaṭiyāl nāṉum avviṭattiliruntu māli cūṭṭa nirākarittāl uṉakkup periya maṉastāpamum avamāṉamum nēriṭumeṉpati niṉittē nāṉ collāmal vantu viṭṭēṉ. iṉi mēl nāṉ āṇeṉpati nī cuttamāy maṟantuviṭṭu uṉṉuṭaṉ kūṭap piṟanta cakōtari pōla eṉṉip pāvikka vēṇṭum. nāṉ puruṣaṉeṉṟu niṉittu eṉṉiṭattil nī vitta nēcamāṉatu nāṉ peṇṇeṉṟu terinta piṟakum māṟāmalirukkumeṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ. muntiṉa rājāvuṭiya puttiriyāṉa uṉakkup pirākiruta rājāttiyākiya nāṉ eṉṉuṭiya rājjiyatti utakatārā pūrvamākat tattañ ceykiṟapaṭiyālum, cakala jaṉaṅkaḷum uṉṉi rājāttiyāka aṅkīkarittuk koṇṭu muracu aṟivittiruppatālum, iṉi nīyē araciyeṉpataṟku aiyamilli. ākiyāl uṉṉuyir pōla maṉṉuyirit tāṅki aracākṣi ceyyumpaṭi cuvāmi uṉakkup pōtumāṉa ñāṉattiyum tiṟamiyiyum anukkirakikkumpaṭi avarip pirārttikkiṟēṉ.

iṅṅaṉam,

ñāṉāmpāḷ

anta nirupattōṭu kūṭa, mantiri piratāṉikaḷ mutalāṉa uttiyōkastarkaḷ naṭakkavēṇṭiya kiramaṅkaḷip paṟṟi, avarkaḷukkum nirupaṅkaḷ aṉuppiṉōm.

cila nāḷāy ātiyūril nāṅkaḷ taṅkiyiruntu pōka vēṇṭumeṉṟu tēvarājap piḷḷi kaṉakacapi mutalāṉavarkaḷ varuntik kēṭṭukkoṇṭa paṭiyāl nāṅkaḷ antap pirakārañ cila nāḷ taṅkiyiruntōm. nāṅkaḷ vikkiramapurikku aṉuppiya nirupaṅkaḷ pōyc cērntavuṭaṉē ānantavalli mantiri piratāṉikaḷ mutaliya atikārikaḷum, iṉṉum anēka pirapukkaḷum, jaṉaṅkaḷum eṅkaḷik kaṇṭukoḷvataṟkāka ātiyūrukku vantārkaḷ. ānantavalli muṉṉē puruṣa vaṭivamākap pārtta ñāṉāmpāḷi ippōtu peṉ vaṭivamākak kaṇṭavuṭaṉē piramittu matimayaṅki mukam māṟi muttu muttākak kaṇṇīr vaṭittāḷ. atik kaṇṭavuṭaṉē ñāṉāmpāḷum maṉam uruki aḻutāḷ. eṉṉuṭiya tāyār avarkaḷ iruvariyum pirattiyēkamāy aḻittu vittuk koṇṭu ānantavalli maṉantēṟumpaṭiyāka anēka uṟutikaḷic coṉṉārkaḷ. ānantavalli oruvāṟu maṉantēṟiṉa piṟaku avaḷ ñāṉāmpāḷip pārttu akkā! eṉakkut tāyun tantiyumāyirunta cakala upākāraṅkaḷiyuñ ceytu nīṅkaḷ eṉṉi antarattil viṭṭuviṭṭu vantu viṭalāmā? oṉṟun teriyāta ciṟu pētiyākiya nāṉ eppaṭi rājāṅkatti vakippēṉ? ākiyāl nīṅkaḷum uṅkaḷ nāyakarum vantu muṉpōl aracu ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟu mikavum nicciyamākap pirārttittuk koṇṭāḷ. appaṭiyē mantiri piratāṉikaḷ mutaliya maṟṟavarkaḷum vēṇṭik koṇṭārkaḷ. ñāṉāmpāḷ ānantavalliyip pārttu rājaputtiriyākiya uṉakkē anta rājāṅkam contam. atil piravēcikka eṅkaḷukkup pāttiyamum illi; iṣṭamum illi. nāṉ kuṭittaṉa muṟiyi anucarittu eṉṉuṭiya nāyakar māmaṉār māmiyār mutalāṉavarkaḷi upacarikka vēṇṭiyavaḷē tavira nāṉ aracu ceyvatu takuti alla eṉṟāḷ. eṉṉa niyāyam colliyum ānantavalli oppuk koḷḷāmal, acantuṣṭiyākavē yiruntāḷ. piṟaku nāṅkaḷum eṅkaḷ tāy tantiyar mutaliyavarkaḷum vikkiramapurikkup pōy eṅkaḷ kiyālē ānantavallikku makuṭāpiṣēkañ ceyvittōm. anta ūrār taṅkaḷi āṇṭa makārājā peṇṇeṉṟu terinta uṭaṉē muṉṉiyip pārkkilum patiṉmaṭaṅku atika vicuvācamum paktiyum uḷḷavarkaḷāy ñāṉāmpāḷiyum eṉṉiyum maṭṭumitamillāmal vāḻttiṉārkaḷ. nāṅkaḷ anta vāḻttukkaḷē parama pirayōjaṉamāka eṇṇi maṉa makiḻcciyuṭaṉ ātiyūrukkut tirumpiṉōm.





* * *





46-ām atikāram

ūrukkut tirumputal—tukkappaṭukiṟavarkaḷ

muṭivil cukam aṭivārkaḷ



nāṅkaḷ ātiyūrukkup pōy oru nāḷ taṅki yiruntu, maṟu nāḷ tēvarāja piḷḷi mutalāṉavarkaḷiṭattil anukñi peṟṟuk koṇṭu cattiyapurikkup payaṇap paṭṭōm. nāṅkaḷ pōkiṟa mārkkaṅkaḷil uḷḷa ūrkaḷil, vicūri kaṇṭu, anēka jaṉaṅkaḷ maṭintu pōṉārkaḷ. cila pirētaṅkaḷ eṭuttu aṭakkañ ceyyap pāttiyastarkaḷillāmal nāṅkaḷ celavu koṭuttuc cēmikkumpaṭic ceyvittōm. ātiyūrukkum cattiyapurikkum naṭu matti yamāṉa cantirakiri yeṉṉum ūr vaḻiyāka nāṅkaḷ pōkumpoḻutu ñāṉāmpāḷukku ammik koppuḷaṅkaḷ uṇṭāki eṅkaḷuṭiya payaṇatti niṟuttumpaṭiyākac campavittatu. ñāṉāmpāḷukku antap payaṅkaramāṉa viyāti kaṇṭavuṭaṉē eṅkaḷukku uṇṭāṉa payattiyum viyākūlattiyum nāṉ eppaṭi vivarikkap pōkiṟēṉ? anta ūrilē vacikkiṟataṟkut takunta cattiramāvatu cāvaṭiyāvatu illāmaliruntapaṭiyāl, nāṅkaḷ kūṭāram aṭikka yattaṉamāyiruntōm. appōtu ammi kaṇṭavarkaḷiyellām anta ūriluḷḷa kavarṉmeṇṭu vittiya cālikkuk koṇṭupōy vittiyam pārkkavēṇṭumeṉṟum, appaṭic ceyyāmal vīṭukaḷilāvatu mārkkaṅkaḷilāvatu anta viyātiyastarkaḷi yār vittirukkiṟārkaḷō avarkaḷ taṇṭikkap paṭuvārkaḷ eṉṟum kavarṉmeṇṭāl taṇṭōrā mūlam viḷamparañ ceytārkaḷ. ñāṉāmpāḷi vittiyacālikkuk koṇṭupōka maṉamillāmal nāṅkaḷ tattaḷittuk koṇṭirukkumpōtu avaḷ viyātiyāyiruppatu eppaṭiyō cila iṅkilīṣ ṭākṭarkaḷukkut terintu avarkaḷ eṅkaḷiṭattil vantu ammi kaṇṭa stirīkaḷukku vittiyacāliyil iṅkilīṣ turicāṉikaḷ (English Doctors) vittiyañ ceyvatāl oru kuṟivum uṇṭākāteṉṟum, ñāṉāmpāḷi uṭaṉē vittiyacālikku aṉuppavēṇṭumeṉṟum muṣkarañ ceytārkaḷ. nāṅkaḷ antap pirakāram ñāṉāmpāḷi vittiyacāliyiṟ koṇṭupōy viṭṭōm. anta ṭākṭarkaḷ eṅkaḷuṭiya antastit terintukoṇṭu ataṟkut takkapaṭi ñāṉāmpāḷi oru taṉi aṟiyil vittu iṅkilīṣ turicāṉikaḷik koṇṭu cakala pakkuvaṅkaḷuñ ceyvittārkaḷ. atu tottu viyātiyāṉatāl nāṅkaḷ aṭikkaṭi pōy ñāṉāmpāḷip pārkkiṟataṟku vittiyarkaḷ iṭaṅkoṭukka villi. ataṉāl eṅkaḷukku uṇṭāṉa cañcalam koñcam alla. eṅkaḷuṭiya pirāṇaṅkaḷiyum āstikaḷiyum oṉṟākat tiraṭṭi anta vittiyacāliyil vittiruntāl eppaṭiyō appaṭip pōla nāṅkaḷ oru pakkattilum tariyāmal iravum pakalum anta vittiya cāliyic cuṟṟikkoṇṭē tirintōm.

ñāṉāmpāḷ patiṉintu nāḷ viyātiyāyiruntāḷ. antap patiṉintu nāḷum patiṉintu yukam pōla iruntatu. vittiyarkaḷ eṅkaḷik kāṇum pōtellām cukkiyam ākum. cukkiyam ākum eṉṟu tātu puṣṭiyākac collikkoṇṭu vantārkaḷ. patiṉāṟām nāḷ vittiyarkaḷ eṅkaḷik kaṇṭavuṭaṉē aḻutukoṇṭu kāriyam miñcippōy viṭṭatu.. viyacaṉappaṭa vēṇṭām eṉṟārkaḷ. anta vārttiyik kēṭṭavuṭaṉē āyiram iṭi eṅkaḷ talimēlē viḻuntatupōla aṭittu mōtik kīḻē viḻuntu puraṇṭu puraṇṭu porumiṉōm; pulampiṉōm; pataṟiṉōm; kataṟiṉōm. iṉṉum pala peyarkaḷ iṟantupōy viṭṭatāl anta vittiyacālic cuṟṟilum aḻukik kuralēyallāmal vēṟu kural illi. ittaṉi peyarkaḷik koṇṭupōṉa anta viyāti eṅkaḷiyum vantu koṇṭu pōkātāveṉṟu ettaṉiyō taram pirārttittōm. antat turviyāti eṅkaḷi nāṭavilli. ñāṉāmpāḷuṭiya uttarak kiriyikaḷic ceyyumpaṭi pirētatti eṅkaḷiṭattil oppuvikkumpaṭi kēṭṭukkoṇṭōm. ammiyiṉāl iṟantu pōkiṟa pirētaṅkaḷi veḷiyē koṇṭupōkāmal antak kolliyilē aṭakkañ ceyyumpaṭi mēlāṉa atikārikaḷ uttaravu ceytiruppatākavum ākiyāl pirētattik koṭukka māṭṭōmeṉṟuñ coṉṉārkaḷ. pirētattik kippaṟṟukiṟataṟku nāṅkaḷ evvaḷavō pirayācippaṭṭum payaṉpaṭavilli. ammi mummuramāyirukkiṟa anta ūril oruvarum irukkak kūṭāteṉṟum, uṭaṉē avaravarkaḷuṭiya ūrkaḷukkup pōyviṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟum atikārikaḷ oru pakkattil nirppantittārkaḷ. ñāṉāmpāḷ viyātiyāyirukkumpōtu avaḷukku eṅkaḷ kiyālē pakkuvaṅkaḷ ceyyak kūṭāmalum avaḷ iṟanta piṟaku avaḷ mukattilē kūṭa viḻikkak kūṭāmalum avaḷuṭiya pirētattiyāvatu eṅkaḷ kiyilē eṭuttu aṭakkañ ceyvataṟkuk kūṭa iṭamillāmalum pōyviṭṭatāl nāṅkaḷ paṭṭa tuyaram ivvaḷavu eṉṟu vivarikka oruvarālum kūṭātu. nāṉ uṭaṉē meycōrntu mūrccittu smaraṇi tappip pōyviṭṭēṉ. appāl naṭantatu yātoṉṟum eṉakkut teriyātu. cattiyapurikkup pōṉapiṟaku tāṉ eṉakku mayakkan teḷintu nalla niṉivu vantatu. appōtu eṉ tāy tantiyar mutalāṉavarkaḷ ellōrum eṉ paṭukkiyic cuṟṟi aḻutukoṇṭu niṉṟārkaḷ. nāṉ kaṇṇi viḻitta uṭaṉē eṉ tāyārip pārttu yuñāṉāmpāḷ eṅkē ammā? eṉṟēṉ. avarkaḷum maṟṟavarkaḷum oṉṟuñ collāmal tēmpit tēmpi aḻutārkaḷ. nāṉ maṟupaṭiyum meycōrntu mey kalaṅki aṟivu taṭumāṟit tukkittēṉ. eṅkaḷuṭiya vīṭṭil ñāṉāmpāḷ irunta iṭattiyum avaḷ kaṭṭiṉa vastiraṅkaḷiyum avaḷ paṭitta pustakaṅkaḷiyum maṟṟac cāmāṉkaḷiyum pārttup pārttuk kaṇṇīr viṭṭuk karintēṉ. cattiyapuriyil uḷḷavarkaḷ ellārum nittiyamum vantu ñāṉāmpāḷ rakaciyattilē ceytu vanta tāṉaṅkaḷiyum tarumaṅkaḷiyuñ collic colli, eṅkaḷuṭiya tukkaṅkaḷip putuppittārkaḷ. ñāṉāmpāḷi niṉittu aḻātavarkaḷ oruvarum illi. ēḻikaḷ ellārum “eṅkaḷuṭiya irakṣaki pōyviṭṭāḷē!“ eṉṟu ēṅkiṉārkaḷ. celvarkaḷ ellārum eṅkaḷ cīmāṭṭi pōyviṭṭāḷē! eṉṟu tēmpiṉārkaḷ. mātarkaḷ ellārum “eṅkaḷ maṉōṉmaṇi pōyviṭṭāḷē!” eṉṟu mayaṅkiṉārkaḷ. puruṣarkaḷ ellārum pūviyarkkaraci pōy viṭṭāḷē! eṉṟu pulampiṉārkaḷ. pāvalarkaḷ ellārum eṅkaḷ pākkiyam pōyviṭṭatē! eṉṟu pataṟiṉārkaḷ. utayāstamaṉa camayaṅkaḷil cūriyaṉ civanta varṇamāyiruppatip pārttāl, ñāṉāmpāḷukkākac cūriyaṉ aḻutu, aḻutu, kaṇṇum mukamuñ civantu pōṉatākat tōṉṟiṟṟu. vāṉattil niṉṟu viḻukiṟa paṉi nīriṉāl vāṉamum aḻuvatākat tōṉṟiṟṟu. maliyaruvikaḷi pārkkum pōtu maliyum aḻuvatāka viḷaṅkiṟṟu. camuttiramum aḻuvatu pōl captittuk koṇṭiruntatu. eṉṉuṭiya tuyara mikutiyiṉāl cakala jīvakōṭikaḷum stāvara jaṅkamaṅkaḷum aḻuvatu pōlavē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.

eṉ tāyār eṉakkup pala camayaṅkaḷil āṟutal colla ārampittārkaḷ. avarkaḷ vāyit tiṟakkum pōtellām, aḻukiyun tukkamum aṭittuk koṇṭu, avarkaḷip pēca oṭṭāmal ceytuviṭṭatu. kaṭiciyāka avarkaḷ maṉatit tēṟṟik koṇṭu collukiṟārkaḷ:- makaṉē! kaṭantupōṉa kāriyatti niṉittu anutāpappaṭṭu lāpam eṉṉa? nām evvaḷavutāṉ aḻutālum ñāṉāmpāḷ varap pōkiṟāḷā? ulakattil piṟakkiṟatum iṟakkiṟatum cakajamēyallāmal nūtaṉamāṉa kāriyam eṉṉa irukkiṟatu? nammuṭiya muṉṉōrkaḷ ellārum iṟantu pōṉārkaḷē! nāmum oru nāḷ iṟantu pōkavēṇṭiyavarkaḷ tāṉē! nammi vīṭu pō, pō eṉkiṟatu. kāṭu vā vā eṉkiṟatu.

kālam ōyāmal ōṭikkoṇṭē irukkiṟatu. atu ōṭumpoḻutu nammuṭiya āyuciyum vārikkoṇṭu pōkiṟatu. nāḷ, vāram, mācam, āṇṭu eṉṟu pala rūpam eṭuttuppōvatu nammuṭiya āyucēyaṉṟi vēṟeṉṉa? ciṟiyōrkaḷāyiruntālum, periyōrkaḷāyiruntālum, ēḻikaḷāyiruntālum, taṉavāṉkaḷāyiruntālum oruvariyum maraṇam viṭuvatilli. cettuppōṉa piṇattukkāka iṉimēl iṟakkappōkiṟa piṇam kattukiṟateṉṟu paṭṭaṇattup piḷḷi coṉṉatu uṇmi tāṉē! itaṟku nūṟu varuṣattiṟku muṉ irunta jaṉaṅkaḷil yārāvatu oruvar ippōtu irukkiṟārkaḷā? avarkaḷ ellārum iṟantupōy avarkaḷuṭiya cantatikaḷ ippōtu ulakattil vacikkiṟārkaḷ. iṉṉum nūṟu varuṣattiṟkuḷ ippōtu irukkiṟa cakala jaṉaṅkaḷum maṭintupōy avarkaḷuṭiya cantatikaḷ varuvārkaḷeṉpataṟkuc cantēkamā? maraṅkaḷil ilikaḷ paḻuttu utirntupōyp putu ilikaḷ uṇṭāvatupōl, ulakattiluḷḷa jaṉaṅkaḷ putitu putitāka māṟikkoṇṭē yirukkiṟārkaḷ. kāṇappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷellām aḻintupōvatu niccayamē. pūlōka vāḻvip pōla aniccayamāṉa kāriyamum vēṟoṉṟum illi. nēṟṟu iruntavar iṉṟikku illi. iṉṟikku irukkiṟavarkaḷ nāḷikku irārkaḷ. nām aṭutta nimiṣattil jīvittiruppōmeṉpatu niccayamilli.

ñāṉāmpāḷ ūril vantāvatu iṟantu pōkāmal, mārkkattil iṟantupōṉataṟkāka nām atikamākat tukkikkiṟōm. avaḷ ūril vantu iṟantupōṉāl nām tukkikkāmal iruppōmā? nāṭṭil iruntālum, kāṭṭil iruntālum, vīṭṭiliruntālum, eṅkē iruntālum iṟappatu niccayantāṉē! maraṇatti yār taṭukkak kūṭum?

talimuṟi tali muṟiyāyk kappalil jīvaṉam ceykiṟa oru mālumiyi avaṉuṭiya nēcaṉ, ’uṉṉuṭiya muppāṭṭaṉ eṅkē iṟantupōṉāṉ?’ eṉṟu kēṭṭāṉ. ‘kappalil iṟantu pōṉāṉ’ eṉṟu mālumi coṉṉāṉ. ’uṉ pāṭṭaṉ eṅkē iṟantu pōṉāṉ?’ eṉṟu ciṉēkitaṉ kēṭka, ’avaṉuñ camuttirattilē iṟantu pōṉāṉ’ eṉṟāṉ. piṟaku ‘uṉ takappaṉ eṅkē iṟantu pōṉāṉ?‘ eṉṟu kēṭka, ‘takappaṉum kappalilē māṇṭāṉ‘ eṉṟu mālumi coṉṉāṉ. uṭaṉē antac cinēkitaṉ mālumiyip pārttu ‘uṉṉuṭiya muṉṉōrkaḷ ellārum kappalilē iṟantu pōṉatāl, nīyum kappalilē jīvaṉam ceyvatāl kappalilē iṟantu pōvāy! ākiyāl inta jīvaṉatti viṭṭuviṭu‘ eṉṟāṉ. mālumi caṟṟu nēram muṉamāyiruntu, piṟaku nēcaṉip pārttu ‘uṉṉuṭiya muppāṭṭaṉ eṅkē iṟantāṉ?‘ eṉṟāṉ. cinēkitaṉ ‘eṉṉuṭiya muppāṭṭaṉ camuttirattilē iṟakkavilli. maṟṟavarkaḷip pōlap pūmiyilē iṟantāṉ‘ eṉṟāṉ. ‘uṉ pāṭṭaṉun takappaṉum eṅkē iṟantārkaḷ?‘ eṉṟu mālumi kēṭka ‘avarkaḷum pūmiyil iṟantārkaḷ‘ eṉṟu cinēkitaṉ maṟumoḻi coṉṉāṉ. uṭaṉē mālumi avaṉip pārttu ‘uṉṉuṭiya muppāṭṭaṉum, pāṭṭaṉum, takappaṉum pūmiyil iṟantupōṉatāl, nī pūmiyil iruppatu cariyalla‘ eṉṟāṉ. anta mālumi coṉṉatu pōla pūmiyil eṅkē iruntālum cāvatu niccayan tāṉē. oruvaṉ taṉṉuṭiya aṉpaṉip pārttu ‘ulakattil jīvittiruppatum iṟantu pōvatum iraṇṭum eṉakkuc camāṉantāṉ‘ eṉṟāṉ. appaṭiyāṉāl ‘iṟantu pō‘ eṉṟu cinēkitaṉ colla ‘iraṇṭum camāṉamāṉatāl‘ nāṉ jīvittirukkiṟēṉ‘ eṉṟāṉ. avaṉ coṉṉatu pōla nām cāviyum vāḻviyum camāṉamāka niṉikka vēṇṭum.

piḷḷikaḷukkuk kacappāṉa maruntukaḷik koṭuttu, aṭittut tirutti vaḷarkkiṟa aṉpuḷḷa tāy tantiyar pōlak kaṭavuḷum nammit tiruttuvataṟkākavē, anēka camayaṅkaḷil namakkut tuṉpaṅkaḷik koṭukkiṟār. nittiyamāṉa paralōka pākkiyam peritē yaṉṟi, nīrk kumiḻi pōl nilimi yillāta inta ulakattil, nām anupavikkiṟa cukamum cukamalla; tuṉpamum tuṉpam allavē! kaṭavuḷi nām kāṇātapaṭi nammuṭiya tēkamākiya tiri maṟittuk koṇṭiruppatāl, antat tiri eppōtu nīṅkumōveṉṟu puṇṇiyavāṉkaḷellārum āvaluṭaṉ kāttuk koṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. ñāṉāmpāḷukkut tēkamākiya tiri nīṅkiviṭṭatāl, avaḷukkuk kaṭavuḷuṭiya taricaṉamum nittiya pākkiyamum kiṭittirukku mepataṟkuc cantēkam illi. avaḷ nittiyāṉantattiṟ piravēcittiruppatu namakkuc cammatamillātatu pōla, nām avaḷukkāka aḻutu piralāpippatu evvaḷavum cariyalla. ñāṉāmpāḷ pōla nāmum caṉmārkkatti aṉucarippōmāṉāl, nāmum mōkṣa vīṭṭiṟ piravēcittu, ñāṉāmpāḷuṭaṉ nittiya kālamum kūṭi vāḻalāmeṉpatu cattiyamē” eṉṟārkaḷ.

eṉakku eṉ tāyār coṉṉa puttimatikaḷellām nīr mēl eḻuttup pōlavum, kalliṉ mēl viritta viri pōlavum payaṉpaṭāmaṟ pōyviṭṭaṉa. eṉakku hitañ coṉṉa eṉ tāyārē āṟutal illāmal ōyāta maṉamaṭivuḷḷavarkaḷā yiruppārkaḷāṉāl eṉṉuṭiya nilimiyum naṉ vivarikka vēṇṭumā? nāṅkaḷ kari kāṇāta tukkak kaṭalil amiḻnti, kari ēṟuvataṟku vaḻiyillāmaṟ kalaṅkip paritavittuk koṇṭirukkumpōtu, oru nāḷ tēvarājap piḷḷiyum kaṉakacapi mutalāṉavarkaḷum eṅkaḷuṭiya vīṭṭukkuḷḷē vantu nuḻintārkaḷ. ñāṉāmpāḷ pirāṇa viyōkamāṉatik kuṟittu cantirakiriyil iruntu nāṅkaḷ avarkaḷukkuk kaṭitam aṉuppiṉapaṭiyāl, avarkaḷ tukkam vicārikka vantiruppārkaḷeṉṟu niṉittu, tēvarājap piḷḷiyiyuṅ kaṉakacapiyiyum, nāṉ taṉittaṉiyē kaṭṭikkoṇṭu aḻutēṉ. avarkaḷ eṉṉōṭukūṭa aḻavumilli. mukattil tukkak kuṟi viḷaṅkavumilli. avarkaḷ irakkamillāta maṉuṣarkaḷeṉṟu niṉittu nāṉ mukattic cuḷittukkoṇṭu appāṟ pōy muṉamāyiruntēṉ. uṭaṉē tēvarājap piḷḷi eṉṉip pārttu cantōṣac ceyti koṇṭu vantirukkiṟōm. nīṅkaḷ aḻavēṇṭām. uṅkaḷuṭiya tukkatti kaṭavuḷ cantōṣamāka māṟṟiviṭṭār. eppaṭiyeṉṟāl uṅkaḷuṭiya tukkak kaṭitam vantavuṭaṉē nāṅkaḷ aḷaviṟanta tukkākkirantarkaḷāy uṅkaḷi vantu kaṇṭukoḷvataṟkāka uṭaṉē payaṇam puṟappaṭṭōm. nāṅkaḷ cantirakiriyil vantu cērntu uṭaṉē ñāṉāmpāḷ iṟantavakiyik kuṟittu vicārippataṟkāka vittiyacālikkup pōṉōm. ammi viyātiyiṉ kaṭumiyiṉāl ñāṉāmpāḷukku veku nēram mūccu aṭaṅkiyiruntatākavum, atik koṇṭu avaḷ iṟantu pōṉatāka eṇṇi aṭakkattukku yattaṉañ ceytatākavum, piṟaku svāmi kirupiyiṉāl ñāṉāmpāḷukkup pōṉa pirāṇaṉ tirumpi vantu viṭṭatākavum. iṉṉamum avaḷ vittiya cāliyilē iruppatākavum, iṉi mēl jīvapayamilli yeṉṟum kēḷvip paṭṭōm. uṭaṉē vittiyacālikkuḷḷē pōy ñāṉāmpāḷiyum pārviyiṭṭup parama cantōṣam aṭintōm. ñāṉāmpāḷukku vittiyam pārtta ṭākṭarkaḷi eṉakkut teriyumāṉatāl ñāṉāmpāḷi ūrukku aḻittuk koṇṭu pōvataṟkāka eṉ vacattil oppuvikkumpaṭi kēṭṭuk koṇṭēṉ. avarkaḷ ñāṉāmpāḷ mikavum turppalamāyiruppatāl iraṇṭu nāḷikkup piṟaku aḻittuk koṇṭu pōkavēṇṭum eṉṟārkaḷ. nāṅkaḷ antap pirakāram mūṉṟu nāḷ varikkuṅ kāttiruntu ñāṉāmpāḷi aḻittukkoṇṭu vantirukkiṟōm. ñāṉāmpāḷum eṉṉuṭiya maṉiviyum marumakaḷum oru vaṇṭiyil itō varukiṟārkaḷ. nāṅkaḷ intac cantōṣac camācārañ colvataṟkāka muntivantōm eṉṟār. avar vārtti muṭivataṟkumuṉ ñāṉāmpāḷum maṟṟavarkaḷum vantu uḷḷē pukuntārkaḷ. nāṉ eṉ kātaliyik kaṇṭēṉ. kavali yellām viṇṭēṉ. uḷḷam pūrittēṉ. uṭalam pārittēṉ. paramāṟputamāka ñāṉāmpāḷip piḻippitta jakatīcaṉuṭiya peruṅ karuṇiyi niṉintu niṉintu ānantak kaṇṇīr corintu aṭikkaṭi māṉaca pūji ceytēṉ.

nāṅkaḷ ellārun tukkakkaṭaliṉiṉṟu kariyēṟi ānanta camuttirattil āṭikkoṇṭirukkumpoḻutu, ānantavalliyum avaḷuṭiya parivāraṅkaḷum vantu cērntārkaḷ. ñāṉāmpāḷ iṟanta ceyti kēṭṭatu mutal ānantavalli aḻutaḻutu mukam vīṅkippōyum tēkam iḷittuppōyum iruntāḷ. ñāṉāmpāḷ uyirōṭiruppatip pārttavuṭaṉē ānantavalli eṉkiṟa peyar avaḷukkē takum eṉṟu collumpaṭiyākap piramāṉantam aṭintu tēka paravacam āṉāḷ. piṟaku āṇṭicciyammāḷum avaḷuṭiya puruṣaṉ, piḷḷi mutalāṉavarkaḷum vantu eṅkaḷuṭiya cantōṣattip pākittuk koṇṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ellāriyum nāṅkaḷ oru mācam varikkum niṟutti vittukkoṇṭu, ñāṉāmpāḷ piḻittataṟkākat tiṉantōṟum tēvārātaṉikaḷum, ēḻikaḷukkut tāṉatarmaṅkaḷuñ ceytu vantōm.

pala nāḷāyiṉapiṉ ñāṉāmpāḷ āṇ vēṣam pūṇṭu aracāṅka pāvaṉiyāka ippōtu kāṭṭavēṇṭumeṉṟu ñāṉāmpāḷi eṉ pāṭṭiyār mutalāṉavarkaḷ vēṇṭiṉārkaḷ. ñāṉāmpāḷukku evvaḷavum iṣṭamillā viṭṭālum pala peyarkaḷuṭiya nirppantattiṉāl oru nāḷ ñāṉāmpāḷ aracaṉip pōla vēṣam pūṇṭukoṇṭāḷ. cākṣāt rājavaṭivākavē tōṉṟiṉa ñāṉāmpāḷip pārttu ellārum aticayittuk koṇṭirukkumpōtu ānantavalli akakkaḷippuṭaṉē vantu ñāṉāmpāḷip pārttu “akkā! eṉṉi vēṇṭi nīṅkaḷ āṇākavē iruntu viṭuṅkaḷ. iṉip peṇrūpam eṭukkavēṇṭām eṉṟu colli iru kikaḷiyuṅ kūppik kumpiṭṭāḷ. uṭaṉē campanta mutaliyār vantu makaḷip pārttu ām ammā! āṇ piḷḷi illiyeṉṟu uṉ tāyārukkum eṉakkum uṇṭāyirukkiṟa varuttan tīrum poruṭṭu nī āṇākavē iruntuviṭu eṉṟār. appōtu nāṉ ñāṉāmpāḷi nōkki ivarkaḷuṭiya pēccik kēṭṭukkoṇṭu eṉṉit teruvil viṭātē! ñāṉāmpāḷ! eṉṟēṉ. itik kēṭṭa uṭaṉē aṇṭakaṭākamum veṭikkumpaṭiyāka ellārum tuḷḷit tuḷḷi viḻuntu cirittārkaḷ. nāṉ vikkiramapurikkup pōṉa uṭaṉē antat turvaḻakkāḷikaḷiṭattilum kāvaṟ cēvakarkaḷiṭattilum akappaṭṭuk koṇṭu avastippaṭṭa pāvaṉiyāka vēṣam pōṭṭuk kāṭṭumpaṭi eṉ pāṭṭiyār eṉṉi vēṇṭik koṇṭārkaḷ. nāṉ eṉ pāṭṭiyārip pārttu antap pāvikaḷi maṉacilē niṉittālum eṉakkuc cimma coppaṉamāyirukkiṟatu. vēṭikkikkuk kūṭa anta vēṣam pōṭa eṉ maṉan tuṇiyavilli eṉṟu colli nirākarittu viṭṭēṉ. piṟaku ūril iruntu vantavarkaḷ ellārun taṉittaṉiyē eṅkaḷiṭattil viṭi peṟṟukkoṇṭu avarkaḷuṭiya ūrukkup pirayāṇam āṉārkaḷ.

ānantavalli avaḷukkup pantuvāṉa rājakumāraṉip pāṇikkirakañ ceytukoṇṭu kṣēmamāyirukkiṟāḷ. vikkiramapurikkuñ cattiyapurikkum vivākam āṉatu pōl anta ūrkaḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟu eppōtun taḻuvik koṇṭēyirukkiṉṟaṉa. anta ūril irukkiṟavarkaḷ inta ūrukkum inta ūril irukkiṟavarkaḷ anta ūrukkum aṭikkaṭi pōkkuvaravāyirukkiṟārkaḷ. appaṭiyē cattiyapuriyum ātiyūrum cavukkiyamākavē yirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉ tāyāruṭiya kīrttip piratāpamum ñāṉāmpāḷuṭiya kiyātiyum airōppā varikkum eṭṭi, cakkaravarttiṉi yavarkaḷ kirupi kūrntu yurāja stirīkaḷru eṉkiṟa paṭṭamuṅ koṭuttu anēka jākīrkaḷum viṭṭārkaḷ. teyvāṉukkirakattiṉālum uṅkaḷuṭiya ācīrvāta makimiyiṉālum nāṅkaḷ cakala cāmpirājyaṅkaḷum peṟṟuc cantōṣamāka jīvikkiṟōm. inta carittirattilē pirastāpikkappaṭṭa palarum inta nimiṣam variyil oru kuṟivum illāmal cuka jīvikaḷā yirukkiṟārkaḷ. appaṭiyē iti vācikkiṟavarkaḷ ellārum vaccira carīrikaḷāy nittiya maṅkaḷamāy vāḻntirukkak kaṭavārkaḷ.

Select Opinions



The following extract is taken from 'Christian College Magazine" published in February 1886, at pages 621—22.

"The first Tamil Novel: The story of PRATHAPA MUDALIAR By S. VEDANAYAGAM PILLAI. D strict Munsiff, Mayavaram, Madras Scotish Press 1879."

"In these days when our vernaculars are at a discount the appearance of this Tamil Novel should be hiled with joy. ("One swallow does not make a summer says the proverbs yet it may be the herald of sunny days, and as such deserves to be greeted by a cordial welcome. Such a welcome must be given to this novel, which, thought it cannot constitute a new literature, marks the coming of a new era.)

"The writer of this novel, Mr. Vedanayagam Pilli, besides being our first Tamil Novelist, has other clims to popular esteem. He is one of the best of modern Tamil Poets Some of his best known works are the following.

24





Nithinul, Siddhanta Sangraham, Penmathimalal Pen- kalvi and Penmanum. Readers may be inclined to question his clim to be the first Tamil Novelist, but so far as we know, the clim is perfectly just. It is true that the immortal Beschi wrote the "Adventures of Guru Paramartan," a book made familiar to Englishmen by the pencil of Alfred Crowquill, and we have also seen anothor little book published some twenty years ago in verse which the author designates a Tamil Novel. But the term will be far more justly applied to the work before us than to either of these. The only other work of fiction which deserves mention is the transla. tion of "Undine" which originally appeared in the pages of the 'Dinavitamani." But this is a European tale. The present Novel Prathaba Mudaliar, was published seven years ago, and is now out of print. (It is a volume of three hundred and thirty pages, admirably written, and full of purity of sentiment and sound wisdom.) It is an excellent book to place in the hands of our Tamil Young men is calculated to stir their thought on many subjects, and reveal to them the weaknesses of our time. The writer is not afrid to speak aloud his views on important questions both social and political, and as they are the views of one possessing much intelligence, considerable experience as a Government official, added to an intimate knowledge of native social life, they are entitled to a respectful hearing. The story into which all these are woven is full of interest and thoroughly Hindu. We follow Prathaba Mudaliar through childhood and youth on to his marriage and chequered events of later years, and he is by no means the solitary object of interest to be found in the story; his wife Gnanambal, Kanaka Sabei and others, make up a series of excellent characters, which cannot fail to delight the reader. We were especially pleased with the narrative of Prathaba's childhood and education; the character of his easygoing father and his indulgent grand mother are admirably delineated. English readers cannot fail to learn much from the author's glimpses of the inner life of a “Hindu” family.

The following extract is taken from "Harvest Field" (a Bangalore Magazine specially devoted to Missionary topics) published in March 1886, at page 267.

A chapter from the first Tamil Novel, "Prathaba Mudaliar" the first Tamil Novel, was first published seven years ago, and has, until lately, been for some time out of print. Its author, S. Vedanayagam Pilli, is perhaps the foremost Tamil Writer of the present time, and is master in Poetry as well as in Prose. He is a Christian, and withal a true Tamil man as he ought to be. This book is an indication of a clear advance both in Tamil thought and literature for in it the spirit of the west often clothes itself in Tamil speech. It is an eminently healthy and homely book and is well planned and ably written.

The Hindu

24th July, 1889



The Late Mr. S. Vedanayagam Pilli,

We regret to record the death of Mr. VEDA- NAYAKAM PILLAI pensioned District Munsiff, a resident of Mayavaram, on the night of the 21st from dropsy. As District Munsiff, he was very popular and discharged his duties with great credit and ability, at a time when Munsiffs of the present type we re rare. He was distinguished also for his independence; he once declined to put up with the treatment accorded to him by a judge, who was then well know for his idio syncrasies, and who is now practising in the Madras Bar. But in Mr. Vedanayagam Pilli's case it was not his official life that contributed to rise him in the estimation of his countrymen. He was an erudite scholar of Tamil and devoted his lifetime to the embellishment of Tamil literature. He was a Tamil Novelist and Tamil Poet. His prose ard poetical works are read with great avidity by the Tamil knowing population. The great merit of his works was the purity of expression, and the moral precepts of which they are so full. His ambition was to create a good Tamil literature so as to suit the modern taste of his countrymen, in which he attined eminent success. He interested himself very much in the successful administration of the local Municipality, over the council of which he presided for several years. The following are some of his well-known productions: Penmathimali, Neethinoolthiratu, Prathaba Mudaliar Charitram, and Suguna Sundari. In his death, the Native community have lost a worthy citizen, a talented Tamil Writer, in short, one whose literary attinments and

whose earnest longing to improve the social and intellectual condition of his fellow countrymen have work for him their everlasting gratitude. The present generation of youngmen would find in his life much to admire and imitate. The news will cuse great sorrow to all those that are acquainted with him of his writings.

The “Southern Star”

27th July, 1889



The Late Mr. S. Vedanayagam Pilli:

The night of the 21st july witnessed the death of one of ladia's sons- Mr. VEDANAYAGAM PILLAI, the pensioned Munsiff of Mayavaram, at the age of 65.He was a pious Christian and a great thinker. A regular student till his last moment. In him we have lost a great Tamil Novelist and a Poet. His best productions: The adventure of Prathaba Mudaliar, Sugunasundari, Penmathimali, and Sarva Samaya-Samarasa Keerthani. In a word, he was the Oliver Goldsmith of our time. His death was felt here as something more than a public calamity. Men started at the intelligence and turned pale as if they had heard of the loss of a dear friend. An object of our pride and our hopes was suddenly taken into the account of grief.

Only a few men deserve the name of Gentlemen.“ and

if the word can with perfect propriety be applied, it is to his great man. His life must teach some of our great ment hat even a happy well earned leisure may be profitably. spent for the benefit of his country if more time be not pent in mornings and evenings in their books and hats.

Letters of Condolence



Madras Christian College,

27th July 1889.⁠⁠

Dear Gnanapragasam,

I am sorry to hear of your father's death. I believed, I remember that he sent me a copy of a book of his, which I have heard well spoken of. How are you employed yourself? Accept of my sincere sympathy with you, and

I am﻿﻿

Yours truly,⁠⁠

(Signed) Wm. MILLER





Madras, July 26th 1889

My Dear Friend,

I have received your letters to which I should have replied but that I have been lid aside with illness. I little thought that your letter of to-day would have contined such sad news. Your father's death is a great loss to you. for no one can fill his place. It is a great grief to me. I respected him much both for his goodness and ability. His writings will continue to do much good, and you and all of us who knew him either personally or by his works will continue to cherish his memory. We know that he has gone home to be with God and now what can we do better than follow him as he followed Christ. I pray that God may comfort you and all your bereaved friends. and that you may exert yourself with God's help and prove worthy son of so worthy a man. I wish he could have lived to have seen "Prathaba Mudaliar's in English (the 1st hundred pages of which are now printed.) But God had better things in store for him and we can only bow to His will. I should greatly like to have a photograph of him if you have one to spare, and I hope you will call on me if you come to Madras. I will say nomore now, but assuring you of my sincere sympathy and praying that God may comfort and bless you and all your sorrowing relatives.

I remin, my dear friend,⁠

Yours sincerely,﻿



(Signed) C. Mackenzie Cobban

The Madras Mail, 11th July 1912.

Prathapa Mudaliar Charitram is also a reprint, by his son, a Tamil novel entitled "The life and Adventures of Prathapa Mudaliar," written by the late Mr. S. Veda- nayagam Pilli of Mayavaram. The value of this publi- cation lies in the fact that the author was the first to write a novel and to supply the want of good prose works in the Tamil Language.

A Tribute to Vedanayagam

Pilli



Sri VEDANAYASAM PILLAI was a great inspired poet. In Tamil he is called as Vara Kavi', ie. one who has been blessed with the divine inspiration to compose poems. This inspiration is not received by all. It is only a very few who are blessed with this Divine Inspiration to give the world glorious poems giving the purpose of life, aim of life and the achievement of one's goal.

His poems are full of philosophical and thought. provoking. They are full of divine knowledge and wisdom. They are full of soul-elevating and soul-stirring. They contin the true purpose of human life, the relation of man with God. They elevate the singers and hearers and take one to the height of Supreme thought-provoking and will make one and all to enquire into the nature of the World, God and His relation with man. His poems are pregnant with meaning and a very few can really understand the real essence of these. These poems are not composed by the ordinary man becuse an ordinaary mind can never compose such poems full of light and wisdom. It is only due to his unbound thirst for God and His full vision which has given rise to these poems which even now inspire many and will inspire. generations to come. The whole south is indebted to this great poet, prophet and philosopher for his magnificient rendering of these and which is a source of strength, knowledge, light and love. We can pay our homage to him in right sense only when we learn these poems and live upte the ideal given there. Though this alone we can sincerely pay our homage to this great sint and poet.

Swamy Sivananda

Rishikesh,

23-7-1955.

Courtesy: Vedanayagar - A Good Poet.

mikunta akkaṟiyuṭaṉ meyppu ceytālum, miṉṉūlil piḻi ētēṉum iruntāl tayakkam illāmal, vikkimūlattil immiṉṉūliṉ pēccu pakkattil terivikkalām allatu piḻikaḷi nīṅkaḷē kūṭa cari ceyyalām.
